# Paragon PR7-S: notes for Claude

**Paragon PR7-S** is the Paragon PR7 family's **studio**: a standalone DAW (AGPLv3, JUCE, a program, not a plugin) with a workflow **inspired by Cubase** and the PR7 family's look. The owner chose the name (S for studio).

| Member | Repo |
|---|---|
| PR7-G (guitar) | `ARYEZ/Paragon-X1` (now Paragon-PR7-G) |
| PR7-B (bass) | `ARYEZ/Paragon-PR7-B` |
| PR7-V (vocals) | `ARYEZ/Paragon-PR7-V` |
| PR7-C (channel strip) | `ARYEZ/Paragon-PR7-C` |
| PR7-K (synth: the studio's synth since 0.9.11) | `ARYEZ/Paragon-PR7-K` |

## The plan (agreed with the owner; one release each)

1. **0.1.0, done: MIDI and instruments** (the owner's choice to start). Instrument tracks (the built-in Paragon Synth or VST3 instruments), MIDI recording from a keyboard or the on-screen one, the arrangement, transport, click, cycle, songs saved as `.pr7s`, undo.
2. **0.2.0, done: the piano roll (key editor):** draw, move, copy, resize, delete notes; velocity lane; quantize; in the lower zone, following the selected part.
3. **0.3.0, done: the mixer:** channel strips, inserts (VST3 effects, including our PR7 plugins; 16 slots since 0.5.1), master bus with its own inserts; the on-screen keyboard removed (the owner asked: the lower zone needs the room).
4. **0.4.0, done: audio tracks:** record through the interface (mono or stereo input, latency-compensated), clips with waveform, trim start/end, fades, monitoring through inserts; ASIO on Windows.
5. **0.5.0, done: export** (a mixdown to WAV; the owner agreed to split automation off). **0.5.1:** fixes from checking 0.1–0.5 against the Cubase 15 manual, and family colours only.
6. **0.6.0, done: automation** (Touch writing; lanes, R/W, drawing and editing; the master's row).
7. **0.7.0, done: sends, FX and group channels** (the owner's choice after automation). **0.7.1:** MIDI file import (the owner asked). **0.7.2:** zoom (H in, G out, Shift+F), Auto-Scroll (F), scroll bars, the lower zone resizable, the mixer's rack hidden (the owner asked; the owner remembered G/H the other way round, the manual has H in). **0.7.3:** vertical zoom (Shift+H/G, Ctrl+Shift+wheel), Auto-Scroll's menu (Page Scroll / Stationary Cursor, Suspend Auto-Scroll when Editing).
8. **0.8.0, done: editing tools** (the owner's choice): the arrangement's TOOLS row and keys 1, 3–9 (SELECT, SPLIT, GLUE, ERASE, ZOOM, MUTE, DRAW, PLAY), F9/F10, Alt+X, muted parts. **0.8.1:** Range Selection (2): ranges across tracks, Split/Crop/Delete/Delete Time/Cut/Cut Time/Copy/Paste/Paste Time/Insert Silence/Duplicate, move and Alt-copy by drag. Not yet: multiple selection, automation in ranges. **0.8.2:** locators (Ctrl/Alt/Ctrl+Alt-click, handles, P / Alt+P, number pad 1/2), tracks (drag to reorder, multiple selection, Up/Down, duplicate, remove selected/empty, double-click to add), SPAN ALL SCREENS and the window's place kept (the owner has three monitors). **0.8.3:** audio file import (IMPORT, drag and drop), the CPU meter, the wide layout (the owner's spanned screenshot: the position screen had stretched across all three). **0.8.4:** Render in Place, tool icons and pointers, lit keys black, AUTO-SCROLL and a CPU screen on the transport, + ADD TRACK at the top, Ctrl/Alt in the whole ruler, the spanned view's mixer, family colours for drop-downs and menus (the owner's list). **0.8.5:** the buffer size (the owner's AXE I/O offered only a small one): ASIO first, SETTINGS explains, RELOAD DRIVER. **0.8.6:** plug-ins rendered silence (offline mode without a new set-up).
9. **0.9.0, done: the Inspector** (the owner's screenshot of Cubase's; sections chosen by the owner): Basics, Routing (MIDI IN, CHANNEL, input, OUT), Inserts, Sends, Fader. Track presets and Freeze done in 0.9.8. EQ later (the owner's choice). Not wanted: Track Versions, MIDI Inserts. **0.9.1:** disable / hide tracks, the left zone's VISIBILITY tab, render's Disable / Hide source. **0.9.2:** the lag (whole-view redraws 30 times a second) fixed, several parts selected (rectangle, Shift-click, Ctrl+A; move, copy, paste, duplicate, delete), the Inspector's width and 5 insert slots, track names large without the instrument. **0.9.3:** the key editor at 200% (SIZE), pencil and scissors pointers, SPLIT (3), 1/1 and 1/2 grids. **0.9.4:** random velocities (LOW/MID/HIGH, Alt+B/N/M, ranges kept). **0.9.5:** importing several files (MIDI chooser takes several; audio: one track or a track each). **0.9.6:** Quick Controls (8 per track in the Inspector). **0.9.7:** IMPORT's choices all in its menu (a chooser opened from a question box's answer closed with it on Windows). **0.9.8:** Freeze (tail sizes, keep/delete the file on unfreeze) and track presets (save, load, a new track from one). **0.9.9:** the click stick (hasEditor asked of Kontakt after every change; the screen's engine reads through the audio lock), renders switching only the sounding plugins, hidden solos, rows without M S REC R W, typed fader/pan values. **0.9.10:** question boxes in the family's look, POSITION and TIME screens, F1/F2/F3 (Alt: docked/full screen), the key editor renamed MIDI editor, SNAP (J) and the quantize value on the header, the MIDI editor's panel one column half as wide, velocity handles. **0.9.11:** PR7-K the studio's synth (shipped beside the app), the cycle bar's gradient, SPLIT's ghost lines, zone windows across all screens, SAVE in the background, quieter parts, Add Track in the right-click menus. **0.9.12:** plug-in windows float above the studio (and pass its keys), the master's row at the top with its own Inspector, only the gradient's three colours (tracks, notes by velocity), the play bar pearl white with a pink glow, the track menu A to Z. **0.9.13:** DAWproject import (songs from Cubase: File > Export > DAWproject). **0.9.14:** the mixer's window shows inserts and sends together (docked: the choice, faders only at first), MIDI import asks to match the song to the file's tempo, the session log (crashes and freezes in a readable file).
10. **0.10.0, done: the tempo track** (the owner asked: as Cubase's). Not yet: the Tempo Track Editor window (Ctrl+T), its Inspector event list, Process Tempo, tempo recording/tap/detection, the smart controls, a signature track, musical-mode audio.
11. **0.11.0, done: key commands and macros** (the owner asked, for an Elgato Stream Deck: its Hotkey buttons press the keys given here). Not yet: the MIDI editor's own keys in KEYS. **0.11.1 (released inside 0.12.0: its macOS test asked for a window taller than CI's screen):** the progress box (Render in Place, Freeze, opening a song's plug-ins; every progress bar a meter), the select box scrolls past the edges, SAVE lights up when saved, the mixer window's racks twice the size. The owner saw a mock-up of parts drawn as mixer channels and kept today's look.
12. **0.12.0, done: the Hub** (the owner's screenshot of the other DAW's: a start window opening on the projects folder). **0.12.1:** Render in Place and Freeze render on a thread of their own (they hung the studio for the owner: rendered on the message thread, an offline instrument waiting for its loaders, which waited for the message thread). 
13. **0.13.0, done: the Tempo Track Editor** (the owner asked: as Cubase's): its window (Ctrl+T), toolbar, info line, ruler, signature display, tempo scale, curve, the events listed (our place for the manual's Inspector list), Process Tempo. Not yet: signature changes, tap/recording/detection, Process Bars, smart controls. **0.13.1:** a review of the whole code (four reviewers, every finding checked; `tests/ReviewTests.cpp`). 
14. **0.14.0, done: chord pads** (the owner's choice; the manual's "Using Chord Pads"): the zone (F4), pads, chord editor, modifiers, adaptive voicing, root key, scale and own presets, overlaps, display, trigger keys, a pad to a part. **0.15.0:** the owner's priority is accurate chords (mostly plain major and minor): keys spelled (all 30), a key's chords onto the pads (minor: both v and V), voicings by name, the circle of fifths with scored suggestions (before the chord track: the owner's choice), the pads on the middle screen. **0.16.0:** the chord track (the owner chose the suggested first release). **0.16.1:** a crash fix (the owner's log: `suggestNext` searched from `key.scale().begin()` to another call's `.end()`, two different lists; **never take begin and end from two calls of a function that returns a container**), secondary dominants named by the key's own chord, session log notes one per line. **0.17.0:** the pads on three screens (the keys left, the circle right, the owner asked) and link groups (the owner asked: as the manual's Linking Channels). **0.17.1:** the Inspector's RENDER (Render in Place as last time) in FREEZE's place, DISABLE/ENABLE TRACK always shown, the key's ten most used chords beside the suggestions. **0.17.2:** popular progressions in their place (what the owner meant). **0.17.3:** Change Instrument fixed (instrument ids fell into the Output check), the colour menu (only the family's tints), note ticks on the ruler (the owner asked). **0.17.4:** the playhead glides into place while stopped and the ruler shows where a click lands (the owner asked: it felt static). **0.17.5:** the MIDI editor's playhead glides too. **0.18.0:** bass and melody ideas for the chord track's chords (the owner asked, after asking about another chord tool's features; our own rules and names). **0.18.1:** MIDI files dropped on an instrument track land on it, snapped, with a gradient preview while dragging (the owner's report: drops made new tracks). **0.18.2:** the preview stronger (60%), its notes drawn in it, its strength a Hub setting (the owner asked). **0.19.0:** IDEAS in tabs (the owner asked): piano chords, guitar rhythm (power chords, chugs, gallop...) and lead (pentatonic, blues, shred runs) for the owner's 7-string drop A guitars (Shreddage Jupiter and Rogue), a TUNING, USE IT onto the selected track. **0.19.1:** the guitar ideas' velocities in Shreddage's layout (the owner's: 1-59 palm mute, tighter lower; 60-119 sustain; 120-127 pinch harmonics and rakes). **0.19.2:** rakes 120-126, pinch harmonics 127 (the owner's split). **0.19.3:** the VELOCITIES panel (the owner sets each articulation's velocity by ear). **0.20.0:** humanize (the owner asked): IDEAS' HUMANIZE (amount, FEEL) and the MIDI editor's HUMANIZE, velocities kept in their layers. **0.20.1:** Humanize a key command (Alt+H: the editor's selected notes, else the arrangement's selected parts) and a HUMANIZE section in the Hub's settings (the key, the amount, the layers). **0.20.2:** IDEAS from the chord pads (the owner looked for it there): an IDEAS key on the pads' toolbar, the PLAYER menu's placeholder now opens IDEAS. **0.21.0:** the MediaBay window (F5; the owner chose the core first: locations scanned, results with search and filters, ratings, the previewer, double-click and drag in). **0.21.1:** tags (the manual's Attribute Inspector and Attribute Filter: category/style and their subs, character pairs, tempo, key, rating, comment, user attributes; kept in MediaBay.xml, not written into the files: our choice). **0.22.0:** the Media rack in the right zone (Ctrl+Alt+R; home tiles, instruments, effects, loops, presets, favorites, file browser, drags into the song). **0.22.1:** the MediaBay window's folder tree (the owner's screenshot: the File Browser with a box per folder: tick, square, empty). **0.22.2:** the MediaBay opens where it was left (the owner asked), tempo/key/tags read from inside files (ACID, Apple loops, the other DAW's iXML; the owner's order: this, then 0.23.0 previews in time with the song (Wait for Project Play, Link to Project Tempo with a time-stretcher), then 0.24.0 plug-in presets). **0.23.0:** previews in time with the song (LINK TEMPO with Signalsmith Stretch, WAIT FOR PLAY locked to the bars, transpose / to key, half / double speed). **0.24.0:** VST presets (.vstpreset) in the MediaBay and the rack: read, tagged, matched to their plug-in, a track with one or onto a track. **0.25.0:** MIDI controller lanes (the owner asked; then, the owner's order: 0.26.0 a drum editor with drum maps built in naming each note's drum, then automation as complete as the manual's (typed values for points, several points selected, the panel's modes...), then takes and comping). **0.26.0:** the drum editor and drum maps (GM Map built in, .drm files, the Drum Map Setup). **0.27.0:** automation editing (typed values, Ctrl/Shift/box selection, the event editor's handles, bent ramps, quick scaling, DRAW/ERASE/LINE on lanes, copy/paste automation). **0.28.0:** the Automation panel (F6: modes, Trim, Fill, Suspend, Show, functions, virgin territory, settings). **0.29.0:** takes and comping, part one (lanes, the clip in front, the record modes, the COMP tool, To Front, lane solo, Clean Up Lanes, Create Tracks from Lanes). **0.30.0:** comping, part two (Ctrl audition, dragging splits and edges, Alt-click splits all, the comp handle, Slip Event, Bounce, Delete Overlaps, Merge MIDI in Loop). Not yet: auto fades/crossfades between comped takes, range double-click glue. **0.31.0:** Render in Place's defaults 32-bit float and the original muted/disabled/hidden (the owner's), whole-track renders narrowed to the range or the cycle, Alt+R renders as last time (Read All lost its key), Q from anywhere to the header's quantize value, INSTRUMENT in the editors' panel. **0.32.0:** hitpoints and audio warping (the first of the owner's four: then 0.33.0 Audio Alignment, designed from the manual's Audio Alignment panel and VocAlign 6 Pro's manual (the owner's link): continuous matching of energy patterns, tightness (max difference), flexibility (how far a stretch may go), max shift, only where both play, sync points as suggestions, protected ranges, several targets; the result as warp markers; then VariAudio in two parts). **0.33.0:** Audio Alignment (the manual's panel: reference, targets, Match Words, Prefer Time Shifting, Alignment Precision; ours after VocAlign: only where both play, flexibility, max shift, sync points (red when unmet), protected ranges; splits at the overlap with 10 ms crossfades; warp markers). Not yet: pitch matching of doubles (after VariAudio), several references. **0.34.0:** VariAudio part one (the manual's VariAudio section: Edit VariAudio, segments, pitch snap modes, Correct Pitch, Straighten Curve, segment volume, the Scale Assistant (editor scale / chord track, suggestions, snap, guides, Quantize Pitches), bypass, audition; our own window opened by double-clicking an audio clip). **0.34.1:** the speed review's safe fixes (the owner asked for a review before part two: four reviewers, audio thread / screen / studio / build; every claim checked): trim read in place, flush-to-zero, undo without plug-in states, caches emptied with the song, the warp cache by size, the track list not on sideways scrolls; **0.34.2:** the engine (events kept unless what they're built from changed: a fader move 8.5 ms -> 0.07 ms per update on 16x2000 notes; clips placed once and mixed in one go in the middle; mix lanes only; unmoving gains in one go; one walk for held notes); **0.34.3:** DAWproject import fixes (the owner's Cubase song: new tracks took imported ids, rack instruments as audio tracks, a second master) and the screen's first part (change versions, send slots, timers while hidden); **0.34.4:** the screen's second part (lanes drawn only in the redrawn strip, ArrangeView's rows once per paint, the transport's screens outside the strip skipped); released inside 0.34.5: its macOS strip test was off by one pixel, CoreGraphics' edges, now `SoftwareImageType`). **0.34.5:** multi-core audio (the owner asked: tracks on all cores, bit-identical output; 2.8x on 4 cores here). **0.35.0:** VariAudio part two (segment editing, tilt/rotate/anchor/straighten range, formants, warp start/end, Extract MIDI, harmony voices, MIDI Input/Step, reference track, colours) and the Inspector's LANES | MON row under RENDER/PRESET (the owner asked; off the track rows). Also: the Mac CI job runs on `macos-15-intel` since 0.33.0's release (GitHub's arm64 Macs had no capacity; the app stays universal). **0.36.0:** Follow Chord Track (the owner asked: Root Notes, Chords, Scales, Chords & Scales; the first-time dialog; Live Input; Shift Chords; Map to Chord Track). **0.36.1:** track names in the track list run up to the meter (the owner asked: the row's right strip was still 180 px from MON/LANES; now 76, `rightWidth`). **0.37.0:** the info line for automation points (the owner's screenshot: relative edits of the selection, Ctrl absolute) and Follow Chord Track's Voicings / Single Voice (our voicing libraries). **0.38.0:** the MIDI editor's info line (the owner asked: as the automation's; pitch typed as a note name moves the selection). **0.39.0:** performances (the owner asked for their chord tool's features, in order: 0.39.0 patterns, 0.40.0 a progression library by genre/mood, 0.41.0 better melodies (density, range, steps vs leaps, motifs), 0.42.0 chord detection from MIDI and audio): 24 patterns in IDEAS' PATTERNS tab and PERFORM on the pads. Not yet: audio (VariAudio) following, the info line for parts and events, recording/step input onto the chord track, chords from audio (not promised); piano/guitar players and Pattern mode; Sections and the remote dialog (MIDI Learn). **0.40.0:** the progression library (157 of ours by genre and mood; PROGRESSIONS on the pads, the chord track's menu). **0.41.0:** better melodies (IDEAS' MELODY tab: six forms, MELODY SETTINGS: density, range, leaps, phrase, repeat, rests, syncopation). **0.42.0:** chord detection from MIDI and audio (the part menu's Detect Chords; the MIDI editor's info line names the selection's chord). That finishes the owner's four chord-tool asks. Then the owner chose, in order: 0.43.0 the channel EQ, 0.44.0 the chord tool round two, 0.45.0 tempo and signature, 0.46.0 the info line for parts. **0.43.0:** the channel EQ (four bands and the Pre section on every channel; the Inspector's EQUALIZERS, the EQ window). **0.44.0:** the chord tool round two (recording chords, Sections, REMOTE with MIDI Learn and modifiers, your own patterns, PERFORM on a track). **0.45.0:** tempo and signature (signature changes on a signature track and in the Tempo Track Editor, TAP / Shift+Space with the six modes, the Tempo Detection panel). **0.45.1:** a click on an automation lane adds the point on the line (the line's value there, or the control's on an empty lane; the owner: they took the mouse's height), Ctrl+Enter on both info lines (`InfoEntry`: JUCE's TextEditor answers only a plain Enter). **0.46.0:** the channel EQ as four bands of the other DAW's Frequency 2 (the owner asked; core + spectrum + Auto Listen, in place of the old bands; the info line for parts moves to 0.47.0). **0.46.1:** a click adds a point between points too (on the line; the owner's wish over the manual's rule), Delete selects the next point (the owner asked). **0.47.0:** the info line for parts and events (FILE/NAME/START/END/LENGTH/OFFSET/FADES/VOLUME/MUTE, MIDI TRANSPOSE/VELOCITY; an event's volume and a part's transpose/velocity new, playback only). **0.48.0:** PR7-G 0.59.0's look on the neon finishes (black keys in neon rings: `Finish::neonRings`). Then the owner chose, in order: 0.49.0 markers, 0.50.0 folder tracks, 0.51.0 export round two (stems, MIDI file export), 0.52.0 auto crossfades for comping. **0.49.0:** markers (the marker track, position and cycle markers, the Markers window, the manual's keys, MIDI file markers). **0.50.0:** folder tracks (holding and nesting tracks, folding, M/S for all, folder blocks, a folder's group channel, DAWproject folders). **0.51.0:** export round two (channels a file each, cycle markers a file each, MIDI file export). **0.52.0:** auto fades and crossfades (the manual's Auto Fades dialog: crossfades at comp cuts on by default, fade in/out, 1-500 ms, Equal Power/Gain, the song's or a track's own). That finishes the owner's four. **0.52.1:** Render in Place's Disable/Hide greyed out under CYCLE (whole tracks stay whole, over the cycle; a warning when parts lie outside) and the session log's freeze report rebuilt (steps under way, every thread without the loader lock, STUCK renders, closing watched; the owner's hangs left logs ending on what had finished), the export pages bigger (`ScaledPage`), plug-in windows on the right-hand screen (`Windows::rightmostScreenArea`). **0.52.2:** plug-in windows remember their place (per plug-in name), PLUGINS in the Hub's look (`PluginsPage`), the process ended after closing (`SessionLog::endProcessNow`: the owner's studio stayed in Task Manager). **0.52.3:** the app's icon (the "7S" monogram; `Logo::drawAppIcon`) and Stream Deck icons in every zip. Then the owner chose, in order: 0.53.0 better guitar ideas, 0.54.0 a DRUMS tab and bass locked to the kick (the guitar tab's STYLE drives them too: the owner agreed; both generated beats from scratch and the MIDI grooves in the owner's scanned library: the owner's choice), 0.55.0 pads that build a whole section onto tracks; and asked (from the PR7-D chat) for parts dragged out of the arrangement as files (0.53.1, before the drums). **0.53.0:** guitar STYLE (rock, thrash, nu-metal, metalcore, shred: the owner's list) and RIFF SETTINGS (length, palm mutes, tightness, busyness, fills). **0.53.1:** parts dragged out of the arrangement as .mid / .wav files (for PR7-D's GROOVES page, the desktop, other programs). **0.54.0:** IDEAS' DRUMS tab (written beats per STYLE, one locked to the riff; the library's grooves) and the bass locked to the kick / following the riff. Then the owner asked for beats generated by rules, then learned from real drummers: their folder of whole-song drum MIDI (one file a song, no section names), sections found (intro, verse, chorus, bridge, breakdown, ending) and checked on a review page, profiles per section. Order: 0.55.0 the rule-based generator, 0.56.0 song analysis and learned profiles, 0.57.0 pads that build a whole section. **0.55.0:** the beat generator (one beat per section, DRUM SETTINGS; the written beats behind WRITTEN BEATS). **0.56.0:** learning a drummer (a folder of whole-song drum MIDI; sections found and named from the drums, put right on LEARN A DRUMMER; a profile per section; DRUMMER on the DRUMS tab plays from it). **0.57.0:** BUILD SECTION (the owner chose it in IDEAS, drums + bass + guitar + lead + piano; and asked for what comes next suggested as the chord suggestions are). **0.57.1:** speed, round one (the owner asked; three reviewers: audio, screen, waits; each finding checked; `tests/SpeedTests.cpp`). **0.57.2:** speed, round two (audio tracks keep their clips, VariAudio's signature kept, the MediaBay's search text kept). **0.57.3:** the spike report in the session log (the owner's clicks at 29% CPU, buffer 2048), the transport screens' shadow and figure widths kept. **0.57.4:** question boxes see-through (the owner: squared-off corners; as the progress box). **0.57.5:** the spike report per plug-in (the owner asked). **0.57.6:** the Audio Alignment panel's REFERENCE / TARGET lanes each with + and CLEAR (the owner asked: as the manual's panel). **0.58.0:** THE COMPOSER (the owner asked, after another idea generator: every layer stacked, each with a lock and a re-roll, one GENERATE). **0.59.0:** groups into groups (the owner's vocal buses: verse vox and chorus vox into John vox, into the master) and a composer lane dragged out as a MIDI file. **0.60.0:** routing as the other DAW's (the owner: any track anywhere but itself: outputs to groups or FX channels, FX outputs and sends, loops refused) and SETTINGS' DRAW WITHOUT THE GRAPHICS CARD (the owner's NEW froze inside the NVIDIA driver; not reproduced here). **0.61.0:** the composer's ROLL a new take of the same approach (the owner: the approach only from the style menu; GENERATE and ROLL keep the progression too), BUSY per lane, LOW STRING (bass, guitar) and a bass tuning. **0.62.0:** the Hub's INSTRUMENTS (profiles: range, tuning, frets, bend, keyswitches, velocity layers, the lane each is for; Djinn Bass 2 built in from the owner's screenshot, their tuning A0 D1 A1 D2 G2; since 0.82.2 A0 E1 A1 D2 G2) and the composer's string menu (any / low / ONLY STRING n with the profile's force-string keyswitch held). **0.62.1:** Shreddage Jupiter and Rogue on + NEW (the owner's: 7-string drop A, 24 frets, their velocity layers; `InstrumentProfile::shreddage`). **0.62.2:** from the owner's Jupiter manual (Shreddage 3.5: TACT articulations, Behavioral Keyswitches C-2..F#-2 force strings 1..7 (latching), G-2 off) and their map (Sustain C-1, Power Chords C#-1): `Keyswitch::forcesString` -1 = turns forcing off, `releaseKey()`, `playOnString (.., release)` presses it after the last note's start. Rogue the same as Jupiter (the owner confirmed: keyswitches, velocities, everything). **0.63.0:** power chords by keyswitch (the owner asked: a root alone after the power chord articulation C#-1, single notes back on C-1; the same velocity layers on both: the owner's): `Keyswitch::Use` none/powerChords/singleNotes (XML `use`), `keyUsedFor`, `usePowerChordKeys (idea, power, single)` (a group starting together of a root, its fifth and octaves: the root alone, the longest length, the loudest velocity; a key only where the kind changes and before the first; strums untouched), in `composeLayers` (guitar and lead, after humanize, before ONLY STRING; `playOnString` keeps the keys) and `LinesPage::getIdeas` (rhythm and lead: the LEAD profile else the GUITAR's); keyswitch notes not drawn on IDEAS' cards; InstrumentsPage's use box items 101/102 (`setKeyswitch` -101/-102). **0.63.1:** the owner: nothing shorter or faster than a 16th on Shreddage and Djinn, 16th chugs rare: `InstrumentProfile::shortestNote` (beats; default 0.25) / `chugRun` (default 0, guitars from XML 2), `keepPlayable (idea, shortest, chugRun, length)` (hits = notes together; a hit under `shortest - 0.04` after the last kept left out; lengths to `shortest` bounded by the next hit; muted (velocity < 60) hits a 16th apart at most `chugRun` in a row), before the power chords in `composeLayers` (bass/guitar/lead) and `LinesPage::getIdeas` (rhythm/lead/bass); InstrumentsPage SHORTEST NOTE / 16TH CHUGS IN A ROW boxes (`setShortestNote`, `setChugRun`). **0.64.0:** GROOVE (the owner's examples: Machine Head's Ten Ton Hammer, Kittie's Brackish: chugs answered by high dissonant stabs): `GuitarStyle::groove` (last; nu-metal's leads, beats, generator rules and fills), six riffs with stab characters `v` (tritone, base+24 and +30, vibrato) / `w` (base+22 and +24 bent a whole step) / `u` (base+26 ringing); `LineNote::Effect` none/vibrato/bend/ring (shapeRiff's palm mutes and busyness and `riffTake` leave effect notes alone); `Studio::partFromIdea` (every idea -> part: addLinePart, buildSection, auditionComposer, writeLineForDrag) and `effectControllers (notes, bendRange)` (bend: pitch bend 8192, ramp to a whole step by the profile's bend range (else 2) over half the note (0.25 beat at most), 8192 at its end; vibrato: CC 1 0, 0 at 30%, ramp to 96 at 80%, 0 at the end). Tests `tests/GrooveTests.cpp`. **0.65.0:** SOLO (the owner asked; a song section, solos by STYLE): `DrumSection::solo` (last; `drumSections()` 7; ride hand; `DrumProfile::sections` 7, `habitsFor` falls back chorus, verse...; DrumLearnPage colour palette (2, 3)), `guitarSolo (leads, spans, bpb, tuning)` (Riffs.h: the six leads calm->busy by onsets a beat, one per two-bar phrase; each phrase's longest single sustain-layer note >= 0.75 beat written a whole step under with `Effect::bend`, others vibrato; the last held to the end), the style overload of `guitarLeadIdeas` appends it (7 leads; the old rock overload keeps 6), `composerStyleCount` lead 7 / drums `drumSections().size()`, LinesPage `sectionIdeas` for SOLO (the lead list's last; the riff through `shapeRiff` busyness 0.2), BuildSectionPanel 720 wide, keys by count, `suggestNextSections` (solo after 2+ choruses or a bridge, once; after it chorus / breakdown / ending). Tests `tests/SoloTests.cpp`. **0.66.0:** solo styles, round one (the owner's list in four rounds, `docs/solo-styles/README.md`: technique sheets researched from lessons and interviews, never their solos; **the app shows our own descriptive names, never the players'**, the owner's choice): `engine/SoloEngine.h/.cpp` `SoloStyle` automatic / soaringBends / rockSwagger / tapAndDive / bluesHammer (the owner approved the names), `composeSolo` (a motif stated, answered, higher, developed; the style's burst in the later phrases; dive bombs; nothing under `shortest`), `LineNote::amount` (bend semitones / vibrato depth / dive semitones) and `technique` (tapping / tremolo / harmonic), `Effect::dive`, `ArticulationKeys` / `useArticulationKeys`, Shreddage's other keys (the manual's: Staccato D-1, Tremolo F#-1, Choke G-1, Harmonics G#-1, Tapping A-1, FX A#-1). **0.67.0:** round two (Dimebag, Hammett, Wylde, Petrucci; the owner approved the names): SCREAM & DIVE, THRASH FIRE, PINCH SQUEAL, PROG PRECISION (`Burst` chromaticShift / repeatLick / sixes / sequence; profile flags majorThird, aeolian, pinchSqueals, scream, upbeatEntry, endHigh). **0.68.0:** round three (DeVille, Mars, Slash, Prince; the owner approved the names): GLAM FLASH, SLEAZE BLUES, TOP HAT MELODY, PURPLE SCREAM (thinner evidence: said in the sheets and the notes). **0.69.0:** round four (Gates, Lowery, Cantrell, Cobain; the owner approved the names): GYPSY SHRED, TENSION & RELEASE, TORTURED BENDS, GRUNGE MELODY. That finishes the owner's four rounds (16 styles + BY STYLE). **0.69.1:** the SOLO box moved onto the LEAD column's title (the owner couldn't see it: squeezed out of the tab row at 1360 wide).
