Next: the owner's questions about the other kinds of rhythm, then bass/lead/piano/drums generators, then LEARN MY BAND part two. **0.85.2:** speed, round four (the owner asked; three reviewers: audio, edit path, screen; each finding checked). **0.85.3:** the owner's three: one take by default (lanes on request), the studio's X back to the Hub (the Hub's X quits), chords on the chord track edited like clips with out-of-key warnings. **0.86.0:** SYNC in the composer (the owner's: find a riff, the band written to it; any lane leads, it stays on, DOUBLE THE SYNC TRACK on the bass and drums, the melody and lead looser). **0.87.0:** DRIVING EIGHTHS (the owner: the rhythm guitar's driving eighth-note chorus; their nine examples' choruses read for their shapes, never copied: This Fire Burns, The End of Heartache, Faint, Faster, Tears Don't Fall, This Calling, Left Behind, Come Clarity (its pre-chorus), Parallels). **0.88.0:** DRONING (the owner's second chorus kind: the rhythm guitar rings chords across the bar, the others carry the movement; their examples Without Me, Capricorn, Clown, Circle With Me (the rhythm guitar part they sent after), shapes only). **0.89.0:** the droning chorus's band (the owner's Crawling and Clown MIDI, shapes only: bass doubling the drone, keys' high fifth pulse or motif, lead octave shimmer, open-hat or half-time drums) and SYNC's chord lookups fixed for sections not at beat 0. **0.89.1:** Circle With Me's chorus (every instrument's tab, shapes only): the lead's pedal in threes, the keys' held third a chord, drums locked to the pushes, the bass's loop-end pulse. **0.90.0:** the SECTIONS row (the owner's song engine overhaul, part one: blocks above the chord track, each a section's name and length in bars, end to end from bar 1; dragged into order, their music moves with them; double-click opens the composer on that block). Then, agreed with the owner, in order: linked copies (repeats linked: one edit changes all), changed chords rewriting the music in their blocks (composer-built lanes rebuilt from their recipe, hand-edited ones following the chords), the composer block by block (the row replacing its SONG strip).

Be honest about scale: Cubase took a large team decades. Say where it stands.

## Colours (the owner's rule, 0.5.1)

Only the family's colours: anything along the **red / pink / orange** gradient, **pearl white** and **pearl onyx** (the dark panels and greys). **Since 0.9.12 track and note colours are exactly the gradient's three: `pr7s::FamilyColours::red` `#D42E2A`, `pink` `#DE0639`, `orange` `#CD6619` (Project.h; `Finish::neonGradient` uses them).** No blue, green, violet, aqua or yellow anywhere (track colours included: `trackColour`, tested). PR7-G's signal-chain colours were removed from `ParagonColours`. The shared `LedDisplay` still has the plugins' green "in tune" for a tuner, which PR7-S never shows.

## The Cubase manual (0.5.1)

The owner wants Cubase **15**'s behaviour. Its manual is the **Cubase Pro 15 Help** on www.steinberg.help (allowed in this environment's network; archive.steinberg.help isn't). It's a Fluid Topics site with a JSON API:
- `curl https://www.steinberg.help/api/khub/maps` lists the manuals (Cubase Pro Help 15.0.30, English: map id `NlutMEjaYjAqk1bhdcAc5g`).
- `.../api/khub/maps/<map id>/toc` is the table of contents (nodes with `title`, `contentId`, `children`).
- `.../api/khub/maps/<map id>/topics/<contentId>/content` is a page's HTML.

Read the chapter before building a feature "as Cubase does", and quote it. 0.5.1 checked 0.1–0.5 against it. Still different, and left so on purpose:
- **Keys (the owner's choice: they stay):** R records (Cubase: R arms, Num * records), / cycles (Cubase: Num /), Home goes to the start (Cubase: Num . or ,), Escape closes the key editor (Cubase: Return opens/closes the editor).
- Only one track armed at a time; the export's "include the click" (our addition); the "<song> Audio" folder.

Cubase defaults it confirmed: Space, C, Delete, Ctrl+D, Ctrl+Z/Shift+Z, Ctrl+N/O/S/Shift+S, Ctrl+E, F3 (MixConsole), Alt+F3 (in the lower zone), Q, the key editor's tool keys 1/5/8, 120 ticks per sixteenth, middle C = C3, +6 dB fader top, the balance pan.

## Names

**Never** "Cubase", "Steinberg" or their graphics in the app, repo or release notes. "Inspired by Cubase's workflow" is fine. The look is the PR7 family's own.

**One exception (the owner agreed, 0.4.0):** ASIO's trademark credit, "ASIO is a trademark and software of Steinberg Media Technologies GmbH", in the release notes' install text, the README, and where ASIO is mentioned. Nothing more.

## Code layout

- `source/engine/`: no screen needed, fully testable.
  - `Project`: the song as plain data (tempo, signature, cycle, click, tracks, parts, notes in **beats** = quarter notes, each track's `inserts`, the master's volume and `masterInserts`), XML save/open, bar/beat formatting.
  - `Engine`: the audio device callback. Plays each track's events through its instrument (`AudioPluginInstance`), the cycle, the click, mix (volume, balance pan, mute, solo), live MIDI (devices via `MidiMessageCollector`, the on-screen `MidiKeyboardState`) into the live track, and records note on/off with their beats into a lock-free FIFO. **Threads:** the audio thread only `ScopedTryLock`s (a missed lock = one silent block); the message thread prepares everything outside the lock and swaps under it (`update`, `setInstrument`, `removeTrack`). No allocation on the audio thread. Signal path: instrument, its inserts in order, fader and pan, into the master buffer; master inserts, master fader, output; the click after the master. Effects are prepared stereo if they allow it, else as they are (mono in: L+R mixed; mono out: to both). Inserts by `insertEffect` / `removeEffect` (bus id 0 = master): 16 slots in fixed places (an `Insert` can be empty: `isEmpty()`; none after the last used), the first `preFaderSlots` before the fader and pan, the rest after (per track, and `masterPreFaderSlots`); the fader is applied to the track's own buffer, then the post-fader inserts, then the sum. Do sample work on raw pointers (FloatVectorOperations), not `AudioBuffer` methods: a buffer remembers being cleared and its own methods then skip work, even after something wrote to it through another view (it silenced mono effects once). Meters have two readers (0: transport and track list, 1: the mixer) so they don't take each other's peaks.
  - **Audio tracks:** `Track::Kind::audio` (input, stereoInput, monitor); their parts are clips (`Part::audioFile`, `audioOffset` seconds, `fadeIn`/`fadeOut` seconds; start/length in beats). `Engine::addAudioTrack`, clips played sample-accurately per segment of the block (`playClips`), input monitoring through the track's inserts, recording through a `ThreadedWriter` set with `setRecorder` (written on the audio thread, the first sample's song position in `getAudioRecordStart`; in a cycle every pass goes to the file and the clip plays the last complete one, as Cubase's Replace mode). `Studio`: recordings in `getAudioFolder()` ("<song> Audio" beside the song, else Documents/Paragon PR7-S/Unsaved Recordings/<time>; save copies them over), loaded whole and resampled to the engine's rate (`getAudio`, with peaks for drawing), latency compensation from the device (`setLatencyForTests`). `Engine::render` takes an input buffer for tests. Moving the position while stopped sets `rebase` (the clock restarts there even if play comes before a block).
  - **Export:** `Studio::beginExport` / `renderExport` / `endExport` (`exportMix` does all three). Begin takes the engine off the interface, re-prepares it at the export's rate, plays a copy of the song (no cycle, the click as asked, parts cut at the end so the ring-out holds only what's sounding; the ring-out goes on until 0.25 s below -100 dB, up to `tailSeconds`: 30 for the whole song, 0 for the cycle; 16, 24 or 32-bit float) and sets every plugin non-realtime (`Engine::setNonRealtime`); render runs on any thread (since 0.13.1 through `exportMix` and the renderRunner, as Render in Place); end puts everything back and deletes an unfinished file. `Windows::ExportPage` asks what (tests drive it directly; it's shown as a modal `DialogWindow`). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_EXPORT=1` draws it over the snapshot.
  - **Automation (0.6.0):** `AutomationLane` (points: beat and value 0–1, the control's travel; volume's is the fader's law `volumeToTravel`; mute steps, the rest ramp), on tracks and the master; R/W per track (`automationRead`/`Write`; W turns R on), Mute Automation per lane. The engine plays lanes with R on: plugin knobs once a block before the plugin, volume/pan/mute every 32 samples ramped, at the playhead when stopped; `getMixNow` for the faders. Studio: show/hide, append, `setLaneControl` (non-destructive: the old control's points kept hidden), remove, mute, points (turns R on), R/W all. Lane edits are undo steps; undo leaves what's shown and R/W. Removing or replacing an insert takes its lanes (from the undo history too). The manual's chapter: `Automation` in the TOC. **Writing:** `grabControl` / `releaseControl` (the UI calls them on a fader's or knob's drag start and end; mute's click does both), values through `setVolume`/`setPan`/`setMute`/`setMasterVolume`/`setKnob`; a `WritePass` per control; `Engine::setHeld` keeps a held control's lane out of playback; punch out on release (Touch: back to what was there over `returnSeconds`), stop, W off, a jump, the cycle coming round (`Engine::getCycleCount`); mute latches until stop. Plugin knobs: a `KnobWatcher` (AudioProcessorListener) per plugin, removed before the plugin is deleted (`unwatch` in effectGoing, instrument changes, track removal, new/open). **On screen:** `Timeline::layout` (rows: track, its shown lanes, MASTER and its lanes; laneHeight 48, masterHeight 40, our choice) for both views; `AutomationKeys` (R pink, W red, writing orange: only on the plain white finish; neon keys light pale gold as the other keys do); TrackList `LaneRow` / `MasterRow` / `Arrow`; ArrangeView paints lanes and edits points (`Drag::points/band/draw`, `PointSelection`, `commitPoints`: one undo step); MainView's header R/W, Alt+R/W, Delete for points, Esc to cancel. The master's row is our design (the manual doesn't describe an output channel track).
  - **Sends, FX and group channels (0.7.0):** `Track::Kind::fx` / `group` (`isChannel()`: no parts, no instrument, never armed). `Track::output` (0 master, or a group or FX channel's id; since 0.60.0 any track that makes sound to any group or FX channel, never in a loop: `Project::canRouteTo`), `Track::sends` (up to `Project::maxSends` 8, by slot: `Send` target FX id, levelDb, on, preFader) and `sendsBypass`. Engine: `addChannel`; roles source/group/fx processed in that order; each channel's `bus` collects what's routed or sent to it; pre-fader sends after the pre-fader inserts, post-fader after the fader, pan and post inserts (`runSends`, gain ramped per block); solo of a track never silences channels. Automation `Target::send` (slot = send). Studio: `addFxChannel` ("FX n-<effect>"), `addGroupChannel`, `setOutput`, `setSend`, `setSendLevel` (writes automation like a fader), `removeSend`, `setSendsBypass`; removing a channel resets what pointed at it; `Project::fromXml` resets routing that leads nowhere. UI: add-track menu, channel rows, track menu Output; MixerView INSERTS/SENDS rack toggle (our design), `SendSlot`, OUT button. Manual pages: Send Effects, Effect Channel Tracks, Group Channel Tracks (TOC). Defaults that are our choice: 8 sends, new send post-fader at 0 dB.
  - **MIDI file import (0.7.1):** `MidiImport.h/.cpp`: `readMidiFile` (JUCE `MidiFile`; type 0 split by channel; ticks per quarter or SMPTE; the first tempo and time signature; tracks without notes left out). `Studio::importMidi` (new song: the file's tempo and signature, the empty default track removed, no undo before it; else into this song at a beat, its tempo kept): a Paragon Synth track per imported track, one part over whole bars. MainView: IMPORT button (asks new song or this one when the song has parts) and `FileDragAndDropTarget` (a .mid at the bar dropped on). Manual pages: Importing Audio and MIDI Files > MIDI File Import. Defaults that are our choice: see the 0.7.1 notes.
  - `ParagonSynth`: the built-in instrument (an `AudioPluginInstance`, so it's played like a VST3).
  - `PluginHost`: VST3 format manager and the known-plugin list (kept in the settings file); `getInstruments` / `getEffects`, `create` / `createEffect`.
  - `Project` also has `quantize` (note starts to the *song's* grid; same-pitch twins merge) and `noteName` (middle C = C3, as in Cubase).
  - `Studio`: message-thread controller; owns project, engine, host, `AudioDeviceManager`, settings; every action; turns recordings into parts; gives every part an `id` (not saved; `findPart`) so the key editor keeps its part when indices change; `setNotes` replaces a part's notes (one undo step per editor gesture); the mixer's actions (`addInsert`, `replaceInsert`, `removeInsert`, `setInsertBypass`, `setMasterVolume`; `onEffectGoing` closes an effect's window before it's deleted; `setVolume`/`setPan` with `undoable = false` while a fader is dragged); adding a track records an undo state (so the next change to it can be undone); undo (parts, song settings, track names and mix; not adding/removing tracks); `onInstrumentGoing` lets the UI close an instrument's window **before** the instance is deleted (a plugin editor must never outlive its processor).
- `source/ui/`: `MainView` (header, `TransportBar`, `TrackList` | `ArrangeView`, and the lower zone below them: `KeyEditor` or `MixerView`, one docked at a time, either in its own window instead; MainView's `Zone`s handle both; keys go to the editor after a click in it; no on-screen keyboard since 0.3.0), `Windows` (instrument editor, settings, plugins), `Pearl.h` (the pearl unit surface). From PR7-C: `ParagonLookAndFeel`, `Finish` (settings file "Paragon PR7-S"), `LedDisplay`, `Logo` (the mark with "-S"), `LevelMeter`.
  - `MixerView`: a `Strip` per track and the master's (16 insert slots, pan, M/S/R/W, fader following automation while R is on, with the PR7 fader look lit from the bottom, meter). Insert menus are async popups (not testable directly; the studio actions are).
  - `LevelMeter`: PR7-C's meter (segments every 3 px, `ParagonColours::meterColour` orange to pink-red, held peak 1.5 s), dBFS -60 to +6. `paintSegments` and `Ballistics` are shared by the transport's OUT, the track list (horizontal) and the mixer.
  - `Windows::ZoneWindow`: the key editor or the mixer in its own window (WINDOW / DOCK). Since 0.9.10 (the owner's keys): F2 / F3 open or close the MIDI editor / mixer where they were, Alt+F2 / Alt+F3 move them between docked and full screen (`toggleZone` / `toggleZonePlace`); `toggleMixerWindow` / `toggleMixerDocked` remain for code and tests. Shows the editor without owning it; MainView moves it in and out (`setKeyEditorInWindow`), remembers the choice and the window's state in the settings file. The window is never deleted inside its own click (callAsync).
  - **Ctrl in tests is `commandModifier`** (the app reads Ctrl with `isCommandDown()`: Cmd on a Mac). `ctrlModifier` is the Mac's Control key: 0.32.0's first build failed on macOS only for that.
  - Tests with a real window: don't expect sizes bigger than the screen (macOS keeps a window within the screen's height; CI's screens are small). 0.2.1 failed on macOS CI for that and was never released.
  - Tests on Linux register the window manager's atoms first (`tests/TestMain.cpp`): with no window manager on the virtual display, JUCE fails creating a real window.
  - **Getting around (0.7.2):** `Timeline::autoScroll` / `scrollLength` (8 bars past the last part, at least 32: our choice) / `zoomStep` (1.5, our choice); `ArrangeView::zoom` / `zoomFull` / `followPlayhead` (Page Scroll: playing, or a jump while stopped); the key editor has its own (`KeyEditor::zoom`, `setAutoScroll`, its scroll bars inside it: `scrollBarSize`). MainView: G/H/F/Shift+F routed as the other keys (to the editor after a click in it), the arrangement's scroll bars (`refreshScrollBars`, `scrollBarMoved`), `ZoneDivider` and `setDockedHeight` (each zone's `dockedHeight`, settings `keyEditorHeight` / `mixerHeight`, between `minimumHeight` and `minimumArrangeHeight`); TrackList's AUTO-SCROLL key; settings `arrangeAutoScroll`, `keyEditorAutoScroll`, `mixerRack`. MixerView `setRackShown` (the lit INSERTS/SENDS clicked again), `getMinimumHeight`, `onMinimumHeightChange`. `ParagonLookAndFeel::drawScrollbar` (JUCE's own is blue). **0.7.3:** `AutoScroll` (on, stationary, suspendWhenEditing, suspended; `follow`, `toggle`, `refreshKey`, `showMenu`, `load`/`save` with prefix "arrange" / "keyEditor") in `Timeline::autoScroll` and the key editor; a click in the event display while playing suspends (ArrangeView/KeyEditor `mouseDown`), `followPlayhead` clears it when stopped. Vertical zoom: `Timeline::rowZoom` (0.5 to 3, step 1.25; `trackRowHeight` / `laneRowHeight` / `masterRowHeight`; `layout` and `contentHeight` are members now: use `timeline.layout (studio)`), `ArrangeView::zoomRows`; a track row under 66 px shows one line (TrackList::Row). Key editor `rowHeight` (6 to 36), `zoomRows`. `ParagonLookAndFeel::menuArrowProperty` draws a menu key's arrow (the fonts have none). Zooms aren't saved. Manual pages: Zooming in the Project Window, Zoom Submenu, Auto-Scroll Settings Menu, Suspend Auto-Scroll When Editing, Setting the Track Height.
  - **Editing tools (0.8.0):** `Part::muted` (saved; the engine skips muted parts' notes and clips). Studio `splitPart` (MIDI notes across the cut split in two: the manual's Split MIDI Events, our choice; an audio clip's second half's `audioOffset` carries on, fade-in with the first, fade-out with the second), `splitPartRepeatedly`, `splitAtCursor` (all parts the playhead is in, or one part id), `glueParts` (to the next by start, or all after; MIDI: the heard notes in place; audio: only a clip and the next cut from the same file end to end, else false), `setPartMuted` / `setPartsMuted`, `deletePartsFrom`; each one undo step. `ArrangeView::Tool` (`setTool`, `nextTool`, `toolDown` / `toolUp`, Drag `newPart` / `muteBand` / `zoomBand` / `play`, the split line `hover`), `ArrangeTools` (the row; MainView `toolbarHeight` 32). SELECT's Alt-click splits (no drag), Alt-drag on an empty instrument track draws. MainView routes 1, 3–9 (not after a click in the key editor: its own 1/5/8), F9/F10, Alt+X. The docked mixer's default height is at least `MixerView::getPreferredHeight` until dragged. Manual pages: Project Window Toolbar, Splitting Events (and Repeatedly), Gluing Events Together, Muting Events, Removing Events, Creating Empty MIDI Parts, Selecting with the Object Selection Tool. Our design: PLAY plays the song while held and returns; the Erase tool's Alt (all after it); MUTE's rectangle mutes all it touches unless all are muted.
  - **Range Selection (0.8.1):** `Studio::Range` (start, end, trackIds) and `RangeClip` (per track, parts from the range's start; origin); `splitRange`, `cropRange`, `deleteRange` (closeGap: Delete Time), `copyRange`, `cutRange`, `insertSilence`, `pasteRange` (from a track down, audio only onto audio tracks; `insertTime`: Paste Time), `moveRange` (copy: Alt), `duplicateRange`; helpers `splitTrackAt`, `slice`, `shiftFrom` (all through `cutPart`, so notes across an edge split). ArrangeView `Tool::range`, `range` / `setRange`, `rangeCommand`, `rangeClip`, Drag `rangeNew` / `rangeStart` / `rangeEnd` / `rangeMove`, `rangeDoubleClick` (a part, or the gap), `showRangeMenu`, `paintRange`. MainView routes the range keys before the key editor's (unless the last click was in it); `rangeCopiedLast` decides what Ctrl+V pastes. The tools ignore right-clicks (`isPopupMenu`): the part's menu always. Manual chapter: Range Editing in the Project Window and in the Editors. Our choices: a click alone clears the range; paste on the selected track; automation isn't moved by Delete Time / Insert Silence.
  - **Locators and tracks (0.8.2):** ArrangeView ruler: Ctrl-click left, Alt-click right, Ctrl+Alt both (one grid step), `Drag::cycleLeft` / `cycleRight` (the handles, 6 px), cursors; `locatorsToSelection` (P). MainView: number pad 1/2 (Ctrl: set) checked before the Ctrl shortcuts and the tool digits (the pad types digits), P / Alt+P, Up/Down (the first part on the track if a part was selected), Shift+Delete. Studio: `selectedTracks` with `selectedTrack` (the one clicked last) in it; `toggleTrackSelected`, `selectTrackRun`, `isTrackSelected`, `getSelectedTracks`; `moveTracks` (undoable: `restore` puts the tracks' order back), `duplicateTrack(s)` (states from the running instrument and effects; name + " copy"), `removeTracks`, `isTrackEmpty`, `getEmptyTracks`. TrackList: Row Ctrl/Shift/drag (`dragTrack` / `dropTracks`, the line in `paintOverChildren`), `moveSelectedTracks`, `removeSelectedTracks` (asks if not empty), `addTrackLikeSelected` (double-click), menu items 50–56. Window: `Windows::spanOf` / `allScreensArea` (the strip the screens share), MainView `toggleSpanAllScreens` / `isSpanningAllScreens` (SETTINGS' button), Main.cpp keeps the window's state ("mainWindow"; max 32768 wide). Manual pages: Left and Right Locators (and its children), Reordering Tracks, Selecting Tracks (with Arrow Keys), Duplicating Tracks, Removing Selected / Empty Tracks, Adding Tracks via Double-Click.
  - **0.8.3:** `Studio::importAudio` / `isAudioFile` (WAV/AIFF copied, FLAC/Ogg/MP3 written as 24-bit WAV into `getAudioFolder()`, unique names; clips one after another; a new audio track, stereo if the first file is, unless the target is an audio track; one undo step for the clips); MainView IMPORT menu (`importMidiFile` / `importAudioFiles`), `filesDropped` (audio onto the track under the drop, at the bar). JUCE 9 reads MP3 (JUCE_USE_MP3AUDIOFORMAT is on by default); `tests/data/tone.mp3` (an ffmpeg 440 Hz tone, peak -21.5 dB), `PR7S_TEST_DATA`. CPU: `Engine::notePerformance` (the device callback's time against the block's: an average smoothed 0.9/0.1, the peak since `takePerformance`, `overloads` at load >= 1), TransportBar's CPU meter beside OUT (`followPerformance`, a click resets, tooltip). Wide: `MainView::wideWidth` 2400, `TransportBar::setToolsWidth` (the group keys + position screen `widePositionWidth` 540 + tempo + signature + tools centred together, `getToolsArea`), `ArrangeTools::setOnUnit` / `unitWidth` (62 px keys, TOOLS stamped), the header's keys shifted to the middle. Manual pages: Audio File Import (Importing Audio Files, Import Options), Audio Performance Monitor Panel. `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_VIEW=5760x1000` renders the wide layout (run xvfb with a big enough screen).
  - **0.8.4:** `Studio::renderInPlace` (`RenderOptions` mode / processing / tail / bits / source, `RenderSource` track + stretch + wholeTrack): each stretch through `exportMix` with `ExportOptions::prepareSong` (only the source's parts in it, the others' cleared; Dry: inserts bypassed, sends and output cleared; Dry/Channel: fader and pan at 0 and their lanes dropped; not +Master: master inserts bypassed, master fader 0 dB), clips on a new stereo audio track moved under the source (Dry: copies of the effects with their states, sends, output; Dry/Channel: the fader and pan). `Engine::settleMix` at every export's start (after `setNonRealtime`, which since 0.8.6 releases and re-prepares each plugin switching mode: a VST3 reads realtime/offline in setupProcessing, and JUCE skips that when rate and block size are unchanged; Kontakt and SD3 rendered silence; test `ModeMinded`) (the faders' SmoothedValues, the sends' lastGain, the master: no glide from the last playback; it was audible in renders). `Windows::RenderPage` (settings keys render*; `keptOptions`), MainView `renderInPlace` (menus of part, range, track: "as last time" skips the page). `ToolIcons.h` (paths in 24x24; `draw`, `cursor`; ArrangeView caches the cursors, not a static: they mustn't outlive JUCE), `ToolKey`. Lit pedal keys on neon finishes drawn as black keys (drawButtonBackground/Text: `neonKeys() && ! on`; since 0.48.0 every key is black). TransportBar: `autoScrollArea` (MainView's `autoScrollKey` / `autoScrollMenu`), `cpuScreen` (an LED screen), wide sizes (keys 66, position 440, tools 9x64 with no label) so the group fits 1920. TrackList: + ADD TRACK in the header. Ruler: Ctrl/Alt anywhere. MainView `wasWide`: turning wide opens the mixer docked at its least, racks hidden. LookAndFeel colours for ComboBox, PopupMenu, TextEditor, AlertWindow, ListBox. Manual pages: Rendering Audio and MIDI (Render Tracks / Render Selection dialogs).
  - **0.8.5 (the buffer size):** `AudioAdvice` (engine): `firstDeviceType` (ASIO when installed, used by Studio only when there are no saved settings; JUCE starts on Windows Audio, whose shared mode has one buffer size), `bufferText` (SETTINGS' line per driver type), `typesWithDevices`. `Studio::reloadAudioDevice` (close + `restartLastAudioDevice`: the driver asked afresh). SettingsContent: the selector in a `Viewport` (it sets its own height), the buffer line, RELOAD DRIVER (devices with a control panel), listens to the device manager. Tests: `tests/DeviceTests.h` `FakeDriver` (a named type, reported sizes, `panelSize`), `"[snapshot-settings]"` (`PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_DRIVER`). JUCE facts: ASIO lists min..max from the driver (powers of two if granularity < 0) plus its preferred size; WASAPI shared: the default size only; exclusive: 64..2048; `Button::triggerClick` is async (tests call `onClick`).
  - **The Inspector (0.9.0):** `Inspector` (ui): `width` 210, left of the track list for the whole height (beside the docked zone; the manual's left zone), sections `Section` basics/routing/inserts/sends/fader in a viewport (`Header` folds; settings `inspectorFolded`, `inspectorShown`), shows `getSelectedTrack()`. Fader: `MixerView (studio, true)` (single-strip mode: `setSingleTrack`, no bar/master/rack, meter reader 2; also the owner of the insert/send/output menus and the `SendSlot`s). `TrackList::showInstrumentMenu` is public (the instrument key). MainView `toggleInspector` (INSPECTOR key, Ctrl+Alt+L: the manual's Show/Hide Left Zone, Window Zones category). `ParagonLookAndFeel::barFromCentreProperty` (pan). Routing: `Track::midiInput` (device name, empty: all) and `midiChannel` (1-16, saved when not default, restored by undo); `Studio::setMidiInput` / `setMidiChannel`; Engine events and live MIDI on the track's channel (status & 0xf0 checks), `stopHeldNotes` on it; `Engine::getLiveInput` (the devices' callback: `LiveInput` drops devices the live track doesn't listen to; `setLiveInputFilter`, `acceptsLiveFrom`), set in `updateLiveTrack`. Track meters have 3 readers. `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_INSPECTOR=fader|none`. Manual pages: Inspector, Inspector Sections, Track Inspector Sections (Basics Section for Instrument Tracks), Window Zones Category.
  - **Disable / hide (0.9.1):** `Track::disabled` / `hidden` (saved, not undo steps). `Studio::setTracksDisabled` (states kept from the running plugins, effects removed from the engine (`effectGoing` first), instrument set to nullptr; enable: `host->create` / `createEffect` from the kept states; not channels; disarms; `setArmed`/`selectTrack` won't arm a disabled one), `open` doesn't load a disabled track's plugins, Engine `update`: disabled = silent, no events/clips/lanes, no monitor. `setTracksHidden` (selection moves to a shown track), `isTrackShown`, `getShownTracks`. Hidden: `Timeline::layout` skips them (so the track list, arrangement, lanes), ArrangeView `trackIndex`/`tracksBetween` over shown tracks, `tracksFrom` (paste) skips them, Up/Down, MixerView skips hidden and disabled (our choice: the manual's "sync" always on). Disabled drawn grey (0xff4a454c). TrackList menu 59 (Disable/Enable), 60 (Hide). Inspector tabs (`Tab`, settings `leftZoneTab`), `VisibilityRow` (ToggleButton per track), SHOW ALL, `enable` key. `RenderOptions::Source::disable`, `hideSource` (settings `renderHide`). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_VISIBILITY=1`. Manual pages: Disabling Tracks, Track Visibility, Render Tracks Dialog (Source Track Settings).
  - **0.9.2:** **Redraws:** ArrangeView's and KeyEditor's timers repaint only the playhead's old and new strips when it moved (`refreshPlayhead`, `playheadX`); paint skips rows/parts/notes outside `g.getClipBounds()` (and works out a row's top once per track, not per part); the split tool's hover repaints strips; TransportBar repaints its screens only when they change; the Inspector lists MIDI devices only when MIDI IN opens (`DeviceBox::beforePopup`). Never repaint a whole big view from a timer: the owner's window spans three screens. `tests/PerfTests.cpp` `"[perf]"` (hidden) times it. **Several parts:** ArrangeView `alsoSelected` (ids besides `selection`, the primary the key editor follows), `getSelectedParts`, `isPartSelected`, `selectParts`, `selectAllParts`, `copySelected`, `pasteParts` (atOrigin), `partsClip` (a `RangeClip` from `Studio::copyParts`, pasted with `pasteRange`), Drag `marquee`; Shift-click toggles; dragging a selected part moves all (`Studio::moveParts`: beats and shown-track delta, same kind else its own track); `Studio::deleteParts`. MainView `copiedLast` (range / notes / parts) decides Ctrl+V; Ctrl+C/X go to the parts unless the last click was in the key editor; Alt+V Paste at Origin; Ctrl+A. **Inspector:** MainView `InspectorEdge`, `setInspectorWidth` (180-560, settings `inspectorWidth`); INSERTS in `insertsView` (5 in view, `insertsInView`). **TrackList rows:** no instrument key (hidden), name `titleFont (22)` on the left, keys in two rows on the right. Manual pages: Selecting with the Object Selection Tool, Select Submenu, Moving Events with the Object Selection Tool, Pasting Events, Duplicating Events, Removing Events.
  - **0.9.3:** `ScaledHolder` (ui): the key editor lives in `keyEditorHolder` (MainView's `editorZone` content, `getKeyEditorFrame`), laid out at the frame's size / scale and drawn with `setTransform (scale)`, so everything in it doubles and its own coordinates stay as they were (tests at 100%: `setKeyEditorSize (100)`). `setKeyEditorSize` (100/150/200, settings `keyEditorSize`, default 200; the constructor sets only the frame: the window isn't laid out yet), the editor's `sizeBox` / `onSizeChange` / `setSizeShown`, docked minimum height scales. KeyEditor `Tool::split` (key 3, `splitAt`: the clicked note, or all selected it crosses; snapped unless Ctrl), `penCursor` / `scissorsCursor` from `ToolIcons::cursor`, grids 1/1 and 1/2. Manual pages: Key Editor Toolbar, Splitting Note Events.
  - **0.9.4 (random velocities, the owner's idea, not in the manual):** KeyEditor `randomizeVelocities` (selected notes only; one edit; the selection kept), `velocityRanges` (settings `velocityRange<i>Low/High`, default 40-70 / 70-100 / 100-127), `setVelocityRange` (clamped 1-127, swapped), `velocityKeys` (LOW/MID/HIGH with the range on them) and `velocityRangesKey` (a CallOutBox of three TwoValueHorizontal sliders) in one panel row (the panel must still fit docked at 200%). Keys Alt+B/N/M in the editor and in MainView's `editorsAnyway`.
  - **Freeze and track presets (0.9.8):** `Track::frozen` / `freezeFile` (relative to `getAudioFolder()`, "Freeze/<name> <id>.wav"; saved). `Studio::freezeTrack (id, tailSeconds, error)`: an enabled instrument or audio track with parts; `exportMix` with `prepareSong` (other tracks' parts cleared; its fader/pan 0, mute off, sends and output cleared and those lanes dropped; master inserts bypassed, master 0 dB, `masterRead` off), 32-bit float; then `unloadPlugins` (shared with disable), disarmed, `frozenParts[id]` kept. `changed()` puts frozen tracks' parts back from `frozenParts` first (the lock); `changeInstrument`/`addInsert`/`replaceInsert`/`removeInsert` refuse frozen tracks, `setArmed`/`selectTrack` don't arm them. Engine `update`: a frozen track = one clip of its file from 0, no events, no monitoring. `unfreezeTrack (id, deleteFile)`: `loadPlugins`. `open` doesn't load frozen tracks' plugins, refills `frozenParts`; `save` copies freeze files. `endExport` now calls `settleMix` (the first playback after any render glided from the render's levels). Presets: `makeTrackPreset` (a one-track Project XML with attribute `trackPreset`; plugin states from the running instances; no parts, automation, sends, output or flags), `applyTrackPreset` (same kind only, not frozen/disabled; volume/pan/colour/QCs/MIDI channel/pre-fader slots/input applied to the undo states too: not undoable, as the manual; name and parts kept), `addTrackFromPreset` (a track of its kind, the preset's name, under the selected one), `trackPresetFolder()` (Documents/Paragon PR7-S/Track Presets), `.pr7track`. UI: `TrackActions` (ui; menu ids 70-78: freeze tails 0/2/5/10/30 s, unfreeze keep/delete, save/load preset; `freezeSelected`, `unfreezeSelected`; `savePresetTo` / `loadPresetFrom` / `addTrackFrom` for tests; choosers via `callAsync`), TrackList menu (Render in Place and Change instrument off when frozen) and + ADD TRACK "Track from a preset...", Inspector `freeze` / `preset` keys in Basics (none on a group; FX: PRESET only; REC disabled when frozen), FROZEN under the row's name and on its parts (pearl-white veil). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_FREEZE=1`. Manual pages: Freeze Channel Options Dialog for Instruments, Unfreeze Channel Options Dialog for Instruments, Creating a Track Preset, Applying Track Presets. Our choices: inserts always in, the instrument always unloaded (the manual's options), post-fader inserts rendered before the live fader, one preset folder (the manual: per kind), no multi-track presets or MediaBay, tempo changes after freezing aren't followed.
  - **0.9.9:** **Never call a plugin's `hasEditor()` on a refresh path:** JUCE's VST3 answers by creating the editor's view (Kontakt: slow; every click stuck ~1 s). Use `Studio::hasEditor` (asked once per instance, `editorAnswers`, forgotten in `onPluginGoing`). **The screen never takes the engine's lock to read:** `getInstrument`, `getEffect`, `getNumEffects`, `getSendNowDb`, `takeTrackPeak`, `getMixNow` read atomics and the track/slot lists only the message thread changes (message thread only); the lock is for changes. Renders: `Engine::setNonRealtime (offline, sounding)` switches and prepares only the plugins that switch, offline only those of `sounding` tracks (beginExport: tracks with unmuted parts in the played copy, the channels their output and sends reach, the master's); `Studio::beginBatch` / `endBatch` (Render in Place, `TrackActions::freezeSelected`) keep plugins offline and the device off across several exports; `endExport` settles the mix. Solo: `setTracksHidden` / `setTracksDisabled` clear solo, the engine ignores a disabled track's solo, Render in Place moves a soloed source's solo to the new track when the source is muted/hidden/disabled/removed; `setSolo (id, on, exclusive)` (Ctrl-click S), `anySolo` / `anyMute`, `deactivateAllSolo` / `deactivateAllMute` (TrackList header `muteAll` / `soloAll`, the manual's Deactivate All Mute/Solo States). TrackList rows: no M S REC R W (kept as hidden members), the state written under the name. `ValueSlider` (ui/ValueSlider.h): double-click opens a CallOutBox to type (`parse`, `enter`: one setValue, one undo step; `ValueEntry::parseDb` / `parsePan`), Ctrl-click resets (setDoubleClickReturnValue with commandModifier); mixer strips (and their painted readouts, `Strip::mouseDoubleClick`), the Inspector (readout Labels editable on double-click), TrackList's volume. Tests can't run JUCE's message loop: close CallOutBoxes synchronously (exitModalState + setVisible false), not `dismiss()`. `tests/PerfTests.cpp` has a heavy-plugin-on-an-audio-thread case. `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_STATES=1`. Manual page: Using Solo and Mute (Fader Section).
  - **0.9.10:** `ParagonLookAndFeel::drawAlertBox` (black pearl, red glow edge, white Bebas, `Logo::drawMark` for any icon) and the alert fonts; `"[snapshot-alert]"`. TransportBar: `positionScreen` and `timeScreen` share the old width (`widePositionWidth` is both plus the 12 px gap), `drawFixedPitch` (Bebas figures in fixed cells). MainView: the editor/mixer/Inspector keys are hidden members (Zone needs its button); F1 `toggleInspector`, F2/F3 `toggleZone`, Alt+F2/F3 `toggleZonePlace` (docked <-> window, the window set to the display's userArea); "MIDI Editor" in names (settings keys still `keyEditor*`); `snapButton` / `quantizeButton` (`showQuantizeMenu`, `quantizeName`) after R W, J toggles snap. `Project::snapOn` / `quantizeBeats` (saved as `snap` / `quantize`, not undo steps; `Studio::setSnap` / `setQuantizeBeats`). `Timeline::grid` is the quantize value (a step: locators), `snapGrid` what things snap to (0 with snap off; guard divisions). Pedal keys with `menuArrowProperty` and text draw the text with the arrow on the right. KeyEditor: `sideWidth` 150; the panel's controls in `panelColumn` inside `panelView` (a Viewport), `layoutColumn` (one column, `columnKeyHeight` 24), `InfoView` / `paintInfo` (the selection's text at its foot), `panelHeaderHeight`. Velocity handles: `getVelocityBox`, `velocityHandleAt`, `VelocityHandle` (move, scale, tiltLeft, tiltRight, scaleCentre), `Gesture::velocityHandle`, `dragVelocityHandle` (from `original`, one `commit`). Manual pages: Snap Function, Grid Type Menu, Snap Grid, Project Window Toolbar, Controller Event Editor, Editing Velocity Events. Our choices: grid = quantize always (no Grid Type menu), the key editor keeps its own grid, no Alt handle variants or Stretch.
  - **0.9.11:** **PR7-K, the studio's synth:** `Instrument::Kind::paragonSynth` (name kept so songs load) now means PR7-K; `PluginHost::getStudioSynth` (beside the app: `currentApplicationFile`'s folder, then the VST3 default folders; the newest version; cached; not looked for when `findStudioSynth` is false, which Studio(false) sets so tests use `ParagonSynth`), `create` falls back to `ParagonSynth` when PR7-K is missing or fails; `isStudioSynthFound` labels the menus. CI's release job downloads PR7-K's latest `*-VST3.zip` (gh release download --repo ARYEZ/Paragon-PR7-K) into each artifact folder, `pack` zips the app with `Paragon PR7-K.vst3`; a missing PR7-K is a warning, not a failure. The cycle strip uses `Finish::neonGradient`. ArrangeView `splitLineX` (the snapped line; repaint its strip, not the mouse's). `toggleZonePlace` sets the window to `Windows::allScreensArea()`. `Studio::saveInBackground` (prepareSave on the message thread: states and audio copies; a Project copy's toXml + writeTo on `saver`, a 1-thread ThreadPool; marked saved at once, unsaved again on failure; `saveAlive` guards the call back; the destructor waits), `isSaving`, `waitForSaves` (tests); MainView SAVE / SAVE AS use it; `save` stays synchronous. `paintPart`: body at alpha 0.05 with a 1 px edge at 0.45 (was 0.32). TrackList: `addTrack` hidden; `makeAddTrackMenu (offset)` / `addTrackChosen`, the track menu's "Add Track" submenu (`addTrackOffset` 1000000), `mouseDown` right-click under the rows. Perf tests: saving with large states (~19 ms/MB: toXml a third, writeTo two thirds).
  - **0.9.12:** `Windows::Instrument` (plug-in windows): a Timer sets `setAlwaysOnTop (Process::isForegroundProcess())` (they went behind the spanning main window and looked closed), `onKey` forwards keys to MainView::keyPressed. `FamilyColours` (Project.h): `trackColour` cycles red, pink, orange; `fromXml` maps retired colours first, then `nearest`. KeyEditor `velocityColour` (below MID's low: orange; below HIGH's low: pink; else red) for notes and bars. Playheads (ArrangeView, KeyEditor): pearl white 0xfff4eeea line, pink glow (two widths), red while recording. TrackList `alphabetical` (the track menu sorted, separators dropped; audio inputs in a "Record From" submenu). The master: `Timeline::layout` puts the master's row and lanes first; `Studio::selectMaster` / `isMasterSelected` (selectTrack/toggle/run clear it; `isTrackSelected` false while it's on; selectedTracks cleared, selectedTrack kept for recording); TrackList `MasterRow::mouseDown`, its highlight; Inspector `master` / `showMaster` / `isShowingMaster` (basics: R W and volume via `setMasterVolume`; inserts via `refreshInserts` on bus 0; fader: `MixerView::setSingleTrack (0, true)` shows the master strip, output meter reader 2: `outputMeters` has 3 readers). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_MASTER=1` (chosen last, after the set-up).
  - **DAWproject import (0.9.13):** `.cpr` is closed; Cubase 14+ exports DAWproject (the manual's Exchanging Files > DAWproject Files), Bitwig's open format (github.com/bitwig/dawproject: Project.xsd, the Java model classes; a zip of project.xml, plugin states as .vstpreset, audio). `DawProject.h/.cpp` (engine): `read` (Reader: indexes ids; Transport's first tempo and signature; Structure's Tracks: role master -> masterVolumeDb/masterInserts, effect -> fx, submix -> group, contentType notes -> instrument, audio -> audio, folders flattened (noted); Channel Volume linear/decibel, Pan normalized/min-max, Mute, solo, audioChannels; Devices: Vst3Plugin only (by name and vendor among the known plug-ins; missing ones keep a description and their state), instrument role -> Instrument, audioFX -> inserts (Enabled false -> bypass), others noted; destinations and sends resolved after (`resolveRouting`); Arrangement walked (`walk`/`walkClip`: any timeline's `track` attribute sets the track; times to beats with the first tempo; clips nested and looped flattened into absolute windows; Notes into the top clip's Part; Audio/Warps into audio parts (offset by the warps' first two points; a stretch noted); fades by fadeTimeUnit); Points: mix and send targets into lanes (volume/send via volumeToTravel), plug-in knobs into `pluginLanes` (VST3 parameter id)); `vstPresetToHostState` (the preset's Comp and Cont chunks into JUCE's `<VST3PluginState>` XML, `copyXmlToBinary`). `Studio::importDawProject` (a new unsaved song in a folder of its own beside the unsaved recordings; `openProject`, split out of `open`; plugin lanes matched by `HostedAudioProcessorParameter::getParameterID`; report = notes + missing plug-ins). MainView IMPORT item 5, `importDawProjectFile` / `importDawProject` (the unsaved-changes question after the chooser, never before; the report box, 24 lines at most), drops of `.dawproject`. Tests: `tests/DawProjectTests.cpp` builds a .dawproject (vstpreset, wav) and checks every part; the official library's Java tests weren't run here (outside code), their shapes were read and covered. Not yet: tempo/signature changes, markers, folders, built-in devices, VST2/AU/CLAP, note expressions, MIDI CC automation, stretching, export.
  - **The tempo track (0.10.0):** `TempoMap` (engine/TempoMap.h: `TempoEvent` beat/bpm/ramp, the ramp from the event before; segments with exact ramps: tempo even in beats, seconds by a logarithm; `secondsAt`, `beatAt`, `bpmAt`, `secondsBetween`, `beatAfter`). `Project::tempoTrackOn`, `tempoEvents` (first at 0), `tempoTrackShown`, `tempoLocked`, `newTempoType` (ramp/step/automatic), `tempoViewLow/High`; `tempoMap()` (the fixed `bpm` when off), `bpmAt`, `followsTempoTrack`; saved as a `Tempo` element; undo restores on/events/shown. **Never use `secondsPerBeat()` for anything placed in the song**: use the map (it's only the fixed tempo's). Engine: `Settings::tempo` + `tempoVersion` (the clock re-bases when the map changes), `Timing` (sampleAt/beatAt) in schedule, playClips, the click, recording, automation spans, the playhead's bpm. Studio: `addTempoTrack` (on, one event), `removeTempoTrack` (hidden, off, events kept), `setTempoTrackOn`, `addTempoEvent` (type by newTempoType; at an event's beat: its tempo), `setTempoEvents`, `removeTempoEvents` (never the first), `setTempoAtCursor`, `insertTempoEventAtCursor`, `setTempoLocked`, `setNewTempoType`, `setTempoView`, `getTempoNow`; cutPart/slice/splitTrackAt take the map; recordings, imports, renders, fades by the map. UI: `Timeline::Row::Kind::tempo` after the master's (`tempoRowHeight`); ArrangeView `paintTempo`, `tempoDown/Drag/Up` (Drag tempoPoints/tempoBand/tempoDraw), `tempoSelection`, `editTempoEvent` (an AlertWindow), `showTempoMenu`, `deleteSelectedTempoEvents`, `getTempoArea`, `yForBpm`/`bpmForY`, `fadeInWidth`/`fadeOutWidth`; waveforms per pixel by the map. TrackList `TempoRow` (ON, LOCK, type, the tempo; `onTempoMenu`), Add Track item 8. TransportBar: the tempo at the playhead (TEMPO TRACK), not editable while it's on (the manual), right-click: Activate / Add. Imports: `MidiImport::tempoChanges`; DAWproject `TempoAutomation` or Points aimed at the transport's Tempo (`Reader::tempo`, `toBeats (value, unit, originBeat)`). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_TEMPO=1`. Manual pages: Tempo Track, Tempo Track Controls, Project Tempo Modes, Adding Tempo Changes to the Tempo Track, Inserting Tempo Events at the Cursor Position, Editing Tempo Events in the Tempo Track Editor, Tempo Track Inspector.
  - **Key commands and macros (0.11.0):** `KeyCommands` (ui/KeyCommands.h): `Command` (id like "Transport.Click", category, name, `defaults`, `action`), `Macro` (name, command ids), `Map` (`add`, `keysFor`, `isCustomized`, `assign` (takes the key from its owner, returns it), `removeKey`, `reset`, `resetAll` (macros kept), `clearAll`, `commandFor`, `handle` (a key of the owner's: run it; a default taken away: swallowed; else false), macros (`addMacro`, `renameMacro` (keys and references follow), `duplicateMacro`, `deleteMacro`, `addToMacro (after)`, `removeFromMacro`, `moveInMacro`; ids `Macro.<name>`; `run` stops at depth 5), `toXml`/`fromXml` (only customised commands and the macros; settings key `keyCommands`; presets `.pr7keys` in Documents/Paragon PR7-S/Key Commands), `toHtml`), `describe`/`parse` ("Ctrl+Alt+F13"), `normalise`. MainView: `keyPressed` = `keyMap.handle` then `handleKey` (the built-in keys, unchanged); `registerCommands` (MainViewCommands.cpp: a command with defaults replays its first key through `handleKey`, so context still counts; the others call the action), `saveKeyCommands`, `openKeyCommands` (KEYS in the header, `keysWindow`). `KeyCommandsPage` / `KeyCommandsWindow` (the manual's dialog: search, PRESS KEY, filters, the chosen command's keys with REMOVE, the `KeyCatcher`, RESET, DEFAULTS, CLEAR ALL, PRESETS, EXPORT LIST; macros NEW/DUPLICATE/DELETE/RENAME, steps ADD COMMAND/REMOVE/UP/DOWN); tests use `setSearch`, `searchKey`, `setFilter`, `selectCommand`, `assignKey`, `selectMacro`, `savePresetAs`, `loadPreset`. `"[snapshot-keys]"`. Manual pages: Key Commands, Key Commands Dialog, Assigning / Removing / Searching, Adding Macros, Saving / Loading Key Command Presets, Exporting Key Command Assignments, Resetting Key Commands.
  - **0.11.1:** `ProgressBox` (ui: drawn at once with `performAnyPendingRepaintsNow` while a job holds the message thread, only when a JUCEApplication runs (the tests' Xvfb refuses MIT-SHM); `stayResponsive` peeks the Windows queue (PM_NOREMOVE) so the studio isn't "Not Responding"; `SessionLog::beat`; `paintBar`). Studio `onProgress (progress, what)` (-1: over), `setProgressStage (base, span, what)`, `endProgress`; `exportMix` reports through them; Render in Place per source, `TrackActions::freezeSelected` per track, `openProject` per plug-in loaded. MainView sets `studio.onProgress` (`progressBox`, `getProgressBox`). `ParagonLookAndFeel::drawProgressBar`: `LevelMeter::paintSegments` horizontal (indeterminate: a moving stretch). ArrangeView marquee: anchored in the song (`marqueeBeat`, `marqueeRowY`), `beginDragAutoRepeat (30)`, `scrollTowards` (faster further out). MainView `flashSaved` (property "lit": the plain button's fill accent, text black, "SAVED" 1.2 s; `saveFlashes` so a newer one wins; `isSaveLit`), called from the background save's answer. MixerView `windowStripWidth` 140 / `windowMasterWidth` 160, rows up to `slotHeight * 2` in the window, name 16, send text scales with the slot. `"[snapshot-progress]"`.
  - **The Hub (0.12.0):** `ProjectLibrary` (engine: settings keys `hubRecent` (newest first, 25), `hubUseDefaultLocation`, `hubDefaultLocation` (Documents/Paragon PR7-S/Projects), `hubFolderName` ("Untitled"), `hubUserLocations`, `hubShowPaths`, `hubShowLocation`, `hubAtStart`; in memory without a settings file; `newSongFile` (<location>/<name>/<name>.pr7s, numbered if taken), `findSongs` (a folder and one down, newest first), `templateFolder` (Documents/Paragon PR7-S/Templates)). Studio `getLibrary`; `open`, `save`, `saveInBackground` add recents; `createSong` (newProject + save in its folder), `newFromTemplate` (open, then no file and a new unsaved recordings folder), `saveAsTemplate` (save, then the file and modified put back). `Hub` (ui: the pearl header with the mark, driver and output boxes (`refreshAudio`, "TYPE: device"), DRIVER PANEL, SETTINGS; PROJECTS / HUB SETTINGS tabs; CREATE EMPTY; the location row; search (`setSearch` refreshes itself: a TextEditor's change message is async); `SongList` (sections RECENT, TEMPLATES, IN <folder>; titles fold; `openItem`, a right-click menu); `HubWindow`). MainView `openHub` (HUB key, `hubWindow`; closes once a song is open), `openSong`, `whenSongCanGo` (Save / Don't save / Cancel), `saveAsTemplate`; commands File.Hub, File.SaveTemplate. Main.cpp opens the Hub at start (`opensAtStart`) unless a song was double-clicked. `"[snapshot-hub]"` (`PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_HUB=settings`). Manual pages: Cubase Hub, Hub Settings Dialog (in the TOC under Project Handling). Not: the web links, previews, the test tone.
  - **0.12.1 (renders off the message thread):** `JUCE_MODAL_LOOPS_PERMITTED=1`. Studio `renderRunner` (unset: inline, as the tests; MainView sets it only when a JUCEApplication runs: a `std::thread` does the work while the message thread runs `runDispatchLoopUntil (30)` and `reportRenderProgress`; the `ProgressBox` is modal meanwhile), `isRendering` (Main's close and quit wait; Studio's timer skips), `renderDone` (atomic, written by the render's thread; `onProgress` only from the message thread). `beginExport` / `endExport` (plugin set-up) stay on the message thread; only `renderExport` moves. **Never render on the message thread:** Kontakt-like instruments rendering offline wait for their loader threads, which can wait for the message thread.
  - **The Tempo Track Editor (0.13.0):** `TempoEditor` (ui; its own `Timeline` for zoom and scroll; `Canvas`: ruler (click: position), signature lane (the one signature), tempo scale (`scaleWidth`), the curve (`xForBeat` = scaleWidth + timeline, `yForBpm` by the project's view range); tools select/draw/erase/zoom (keys 1/8/5/6, H/G zoom, Delete); `EventList` (a ListBox, multi-select); toolbar ACTIVATE, AUTO-SCROLL, tools, RAMP/STEP/AUTO, CURRENT TEMPO (editable only with the tempo track off), SNAP, PROCESS TEMPO..., INFO; info line `setSelectedPosition` (`parsePosition` "bar.beat.16th.tick"), `setSelectedValue`, `setSelectedRamp`; `onOtherKey` to MainView). `TempoEditorWindow` (settings `tempoEditorWindow`). `ProcessTempoPage` (locators; seconds or minutes:seconds; `setNewLength` / `setNewEnd`, `getScaling`, `apply`). Studio `processTempo (start, end, newSeconds, factor)` / `tempoScaling`: events in the range scaled by one factor (exact over ramps); edges held by events at the start (a ramp arriving there: an event a 960th of a beat before it) and a 960th after the end (back on the old curve: `before.bpmAt (end + just)`); refuses outside 20-300 BPM; one undo step. MainView `toggleTempoEditor` (Ctrl+T in handleKey's Ctrl switch; command Window.TempoEditor), ArrangeView / TransportBar `onOpenTempoEditor` (their tempo menus). `"[snapshot-tempo-editor]"`. Manual pages: Tempo Track Editor, Tempo Track Editor Toolbar, Editing Tempo Events in the Tempo Track Editor, Process Tempo Dialog, Adjusting the Tempo of a Range.
  - **0.13.1 (the review):** Engine: `TrackState::heldChannel` (notes end on the channel they started on), `checkHeld` set by `update` and `endNotesNoLongerEnded` (a sounding note goes on only if the new list ends it ahead before the key starts again), `releaseNotes()` / `releaseAll` (beginExport: every render starts with notes ended), `effectMidi` 4096 bytes. Studio: `cancelRender` / `wasRenderCancelled` (`renderCancelled`, checked every block in `renderExport`; reset by `exportMix` outside a batch and by `beginBatch`); **while `exportJob` is set nothing calls `engine.update (project)`** (changed(), the device-rate check, `grabControl`, the timer); beginExport punches out first. KnobWatcher: ignores the audio thread and the render's thread (`SessionLog::markAudioThread (bool)`, put back after the render), `unwatch` detaches and deletes it later (callAsync). `copyAudioFiles (from, to)` (prepareSave and newFromTemplate): the song's, the undo history's and frozenParts' files; a different file of the same name at the target: ours copied under a new name and every reference renamed. Frozen tracks: movePart/copyPart/moveParts/pasteRange/moveRange/importAudio leave them alone; `duplicateTrack` makes a live copy. `restore` keeps solo off on hidden/disabled tracks; `sendGone` (lanes and QCs of a removed send); `Project::fromXml` part length at least 1e-6. MainView: `writeExport` = setProgressStage + `exportMix` (no ThreadWithProgressWindow any more), `chooseExportFile` after the page has gone; keys ignored while a mouse button is down (but Space); a swallowed (taken-away) default still reaches the MIDI editor when it has the keys. `ProgressBox::setOnCancel` (CANCEL, Escape; set by the renderRunner). ArrangeView `selectedPartId` / `selectionOf` (the selection found again by id after a change). TrackList `instrumentItems` 100000. TempoEditor `knownEvents` (selection follows by beat+bpm when the count changes); ProcessTempoPage's PROCESS takes a field whose text differs from what it shows. DawProject: `walking` (a content element can't be walked inside itself), `walksLeft` 2,000,000, preset chunk check without overflow, external files ordinary and under 4 GB, `missingNames`, `findPlugin (name, vendor, instrument)` scored. KeyCommands: keys saved as `<Key key=.../>` children (old `keys="a, b"` split on ", " only), on macOS the real Ctrl is "MacCtrl+" ("Ctrl+" stays the command key), `stepsLeft` 1000 per key. MidiImport: a first tempo after tick 0 gets 120 before it. Hub: Rename/Delete off for the open song; Rename checks the audio folder first and moves the song back if it can't follow. **The owner's reports:** `Studio::silenceForRender` (Freeze's and Render in Place's other tracks: parts cleared, `frozen` off and no freeze file, muted, no monitoring; channels kept; a track frozen earlier played its freeze file into every later freeze); MainView `openHub` centres the Hub on the display under the main window's centre (else `allScreensArea`'s); `TransportBar::screenPosition` (figure spaces U+2007, which `drawFixedPitch` sizes as digits: bars 2, beat as wide as the numerator, sixteenth 1, ticks 3); MixerView lays group strips right-aligned against the master; `drawAlertBox` is the ProgressBox's look (the mark at 22,18, the box's own textLayout beside it). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_GROUP=1` adds a group channel to the demo.

Engine `setPadPlayer` (an atomic pointer; LiveInput asks it first).   - **Keys and suggestions (0.15.0):** `Key` (Chords.h: letter 0-6, accidental -1/0/1, minor; `pitchClass`, `name`, `signature` (a minor key's = its relative major's), `scale` (natural minor), `all()` (15 major + 15 minor by the circle), `fromPitchClass` (fewest accidentals), `spellAs` (a pc on a letter), `spell` (in the scale: its degree's letter; the raised 4th always, a minor key's raised 6th/7th; else flats in flat keys and C/Am, sharps in sharp keys, never a double accidental, and Fb/Cb/E#/B# outside the key written plain)). `Chord::name/noteNames/romanName/nashvilleName (const Key&)` (chord tones by letter steps from the root: `letterStep`, dim7's bb7; numerals by the key's own degrees, a minor key's raised 6th/7th unsigned; Nashville from the tonic's major scale), `isInKey` (all notes in the scale, or a minor key's harmonic minor), `keyChords` (seven triads, minor: V and vii° of the raised 7th, the sevenths, minor V7), `namedVoicings` (`Voicing` notes/kind/close: close inversions in two octaves, drop 2, spread; `voicingsFor` = their notes in order; adaptive among close ones; default -1 = the middle close one), `notesText` (octave from the letter), `suggestNext` (`Suggestion` chord/score/reason/inKey: degree tables majorTable/minorTable (our weights), minor v -22, outside the key by root motion -14, down a fifth +5, +3 per common note; candidates: the key's chords, V7, borrowed and secondary dominants). `ChordPads::minor`, `keyLetter` (saved; -1 for 0.14 songs: from rootKey and flats), `key()`, `setKey`, `fillFromKey` (keyboard: white = the seven, black = major I7 ii7 IV7 V7 vi7 / minor i7 IIImaj7 iv7 V V7; grid in order; locked kept); `fillFromScale` major/minor go through it.   - **0.19.0 (piano and guitar ideas):** Lines.h `LineIdea::Part` bass/melody/chords/rhythm/lead; `GuitarTuning` (name, strings low to high; `lowest`, `highest` = top + 24; `all()` first 7-string drop A 33 40 45 50 55 59 64, `named` (unknown: the first)); `pianoChordIdeas` (Block, Broken, Ballad arpeggio, Stride, Octaves and chords, Pop pulse; right hand `voicingsFor` + `closestVoicing`, left hand the root 33-52), `guitarRhythmIdeas` (Power chords, Octave chords, Palm-muted chugs, Gallop, Pedal-point riff, Open strummed shapes (barre shapes on the top six strings, strum 0.02 beats a string, up strokes the top four); every note folded into [lowest, highest]: **Shreddage-style keyswitches live under the lowest string**), `guitarLeadIdeas` (Pentatonic box, Blues scale run, Repeating triplet lick, Descending fours, Neoclassical run, Tremolo picking; range strings[1]+5 to top+22; each bar's first note a chord tone, the last on the last chord), `fifthAbove` (the chord's own fifth: dim b5, aug #5). Studio `addLinePart (idea, spans, ontoTrackId)` (onto it if `canTakeMidi`, else a new track `linePartName`), `getGuitarTuning` / `setGuitarTuning` (settings `guitarTuning`). LinesPage: `Tab` piano/guitar/bass (`partsOf`, settings `ideasTab`), `ideas` array by Part, `makeCards`, tuning ComboBox (guitar tab), `showUseMenu` (selected track or new), `use (part, i, onto)`, `getNumCardsShown`; title IDEAS; colours bass/rhythm orange, melody/lead pink, chords red. Entry points renamed "Piano, Guitar and Bass Ideas...", ChordRow key IDEAS. `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_IDEAS=piano|guitar|bass`. Not: pitch bend (no bends), keyswitches, tab. **0.19.1:** `GuitarVelocity` (Lines.h: muteTight 34 / muteMid 44 / muteLoose 54 (<= 59), sustainSoft 80 / sustain 96 / sustainHard 110 (<= 119), pinch 124; `of` = midi / 127, `midiOf`): chugs and gallop muted, pedal muted, pinch on the pedal riff's every second bar's last eighth and the pentatonic box's last note; everything else 60-119 (tested). LinesPage `play`: guitar ideas without the chords; the preview's chords at 0.5 (64). **0.19.2:** `rakeLeast` 120 / `rake` 123 / `rakeMost` 126, `pinch` 127; `raked (bar)` (every second bar from the first): rakes into the first note in Pentatonic box and Blues scale run, the triplet lick's first note; none in rhythm parts (tested). **0.19.3:** `GuitarVelocities` (Lines.h: the eight values, defaults from `GuitarVelocity`; `get`/`set` by index, `nameOf`, `layerOf`, `open` (in-betweens kept in [sustainSoft, sustainHard]), `toString`/`fromString`), passed into `guitarRhythmIdeas` / `guitarLeadIdeas`; Studio `getGuitarVelocities` / `setGuitarVelocities` (settings `guitarVelocities`); UI `GuitarVelocityPanel` (LinesPage.h: a LinearBar per value, its layer, RESET; `setValue`, `reset`, `onChange`), LinesPage `velocitiesKey` (guitar tab), `showVelocities` (CallOutBox), `setGuitarVelocities` (deals again). `"[snapshot-velocities]"`. **0.20.0 (humanize):** Lines.h `humanize (notes, HumanizeOptions)` (amount, feelMs, bpm, beatsPerBar, originBeat, layers, seed; `HumanizeLimits`: 12 ms, velocity 10, length 12%, strum 25%, together 0.11 beats, feel -3 / +5 ms; timing x0.3 on a bar's first beat, x0.6 on beats; notes starting together move as one; velocity clamped to the layer it started in; repeatable per seed; amount 0 and no feel: unchanged), `guitarLayers()` (1-59, 60-119, 120-126, 127). Studio `getIdeasHumanize` / `getIdeasFeel` (settings `ideasHumanize` default 0, `ideasFeel` default on), `getHumanizeAmount` / `getHumanizeKeepsLayers` (`humanizeAmount` 0.5, `humanizeLayers` on). LinesPage: `humanizeKey` (header, all tabs; `humanizeText`), `HumanizePanel` (AMOUNT bar, FEEL key; `setAmount`, `setFeel`, `onChange`), `setHumanize`, `showHumanize`; `rebuild` humanizes every idea (seed from variation, part, index; guitar parts with `guitarLayers`; feel: rhythm/bass ahead, lead/melody behind, piano none). KeyEditor `humanizeSelected (amount)` (selected notes in time order, written back into their slots; bpm at the first; one commit, selection kept), `humanizeKey` ("HUMANIZE 50%"), `humanizeMenuKey` ("HUMANIZE SET": amounts, keep layers), `getHumanizeKey`. `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_HUMANIZE=1` (the panel). **0.20.1:** command `MIDI.Humanize` (Alt+H; MainView `handleKey`: the editor's selection, else `Studio::humanizeParts (arrange.getSelectedParts())`: every note of the MIDI parts, not audio/frozen, one `changed()`, amount 0: false); KeyEditor Alt+H; the editor's key text follows the amount (timer). Hub: `setKeyCommands (&keyMap)` (MainView's openHub), `humanizeCommand`, `listenForHumanizeKey` / `takeHumanizeKey` (Escape cancels; replaces the command's keys via `assign (.., false)`) / `clearHumanizeKey` / `getHumanizeKeyText`, `getHumanizeAmount` (LinearBar 5-100%), `getHumanizeLayers`; a column right of the toggles on HUB SETTINGS. **0.20.2:** ChordPadsView `ideasKey` (second row, after FROM CHORD TRACK; `onLineIdeas ({})`; `getIdeasKey`), PLAYER menu item 98 "Piano, Guitar and Bass Ideas..." (enabled with chord track events).
  - **The MediaBay (0.21.0):** engine `MediaBay.h/.cpp`: `MediaType` audio/midi/trackPreset/song (`mediaTypeOf` by extension, `mediaTypeName`), `MediaItem` (file, name, size, modified, seconds, sampleRate, channels, bpm, key, beats, notes, rating; `searchText`: name, file name, folder name, type, tempo, key, lower case), `TextSearch` (tokens, a small parser: or (`or`, `,`) of and (`and`, `+`, nothing) of factors (`not`, a leading `-`, parentheses, words; "phrases"); words matched with `contains`; a minus inside a word is the word), `MediaQuery` (text, types, minimumRating, folder, subfolders), `MediaColumn`, `MediaDatabase (store)` (locations; `rescan` on a `Scanner` thread (`scanAll`: RangedDirectoryIterator per location, unchanged files (size + date) not read again, waits while `shouldPause`), `scanNow` (tests), `takeScanResults` (message thread; items sorted by name; saves), `find`, `sort`, `shuffle (seed)`, `setRating` / `getRating` (by path, kept when the file isn't scanned), `read` (audio header; MIDI via `readMidiFile`: beats, notes, its tempo), `bpmFromName` (2-3 digits beside "bpm", 40-300), `keyFromName` (a name part: capital A-G, # or b, then m/min/minor/maj/major or nothing; "bass" isn't Bb), `save` / `load` (XML: Location, Rating, Item); `pendingLock` guards `items`/`locations` against the scanner). Engine `setPreview (buffer, loop)` (swapped under the lock; the old one freed on the message thread), `stopPreview`, `setPreviewGain`, `setPreviewLoop`, `isPreviewPlaying`, `getPreviewProgress`; mixed after the click. Studio `getMediaBay` (lazy; store "MediaBay.xml" beside the settings; first time Documents/Paragon PR7-S a location; `shouldPause` = playing or recording), `readAudio (File)` (uncached; `getAudio` uses it), `previewMedia (file, loop)` (audio: the engine's preview; MIDI: `previewLine` with the file's notes, velocity scaled by the level), `stopMediaPreview`, `isMediaPreviewing`, `getMediaPreviewProgress`, `getMediaPreviewSound`, `setMediaPreviewLoop`, `get/setMediaPreviewLevel` (settings `mediaBayLevel`); `beginExport` stops the preview. UI `MediaBayPage` / `MediaBayWindow` (ui/MediaBayPage.h: title + status (`getStatusText`), RESCAN; toolbar search / AUDIO MIDI PRESETS SONGS / rating box / SUBFOLDERS / SHUFFLE / RESET; `Locations` (ListBox: ALL LOCATIONS + each), ADD FOLDER... (chooser), REMOVE; `table` (TableListBox: rating stars (click: `cellClicked`), name with a type colour tab, type, length, tempo, key, format, folder, size, date; `sortOrderChanged`; drag `getDragSourceDescription` = "mediabay:" + paths a line each); previewer (PLAY/STOP, CYCLE, AUTO PLAY, LEVEL, `waveArea` from the preview's peaks, playhead), USE IT; settings `mediaBayTypes`, `mediaBayRating`, `mediaBaySubfolders`, `mediaBayAutoPlay`, `mediaBayCycle`; test API `setSearch`, `setTypeShown`, `setMinimumRating`, `setSubfolders`, `selectLocation`, `resetFilters`, `shuffle`, `sortBy`, `selectRow`, `setRating`, `play`, `stop`, `setAutoPlay`, `setCycle`, `use`, `addLocation`, `getResult`, `getNumResults`). MainView: F5 / command Window.MediaBay / IMPORT item 6 `toggleMediaBay` (settings `mediaBayWindow`), `useMedia` (audio `importAudio` new track at the playhead, MIDI `importMidiFilesNow` new track, preset `TrackActions::addTrackFrom`, song `whenSongCanGo` + `openSong`), a `DragAndDropTarget` for "mediabay:" (ArrangeView isn't interested, so JUCE walks up to MainView) using `fileDragEnter/Move/Exit` and `mediaDropped` (audio/MIDI through `filesDropped`, others `useMedia`). Tests `tests/MediaBayTests.cpp`; `"[snapshot-mediabay]"`. Manual pages: MediaBay and Media Rack, MediaBay Window, MediaBay Toolbar, File Browser Section, Scanning Folders, Results Section, Filtering According to Media Type / Rating, Performing a Text Search, Boolean Text Search, Shuffling, Previewer Section, Audio Files Previewer, MIDI Files Previewer, Loading Loops and Samples, Adding Tracks by Dragging Files from the MediaBay, MediaBay Settings.
  - **MediaBay tags (0.21.1):** MediaBay.h `MediaAttribute` (category, subCategory, style, subStyle, character, tempo, key, rating, comment, user) + `AttributeId` (kind, user name; `name()` upper case), `MediaTags` (strings, bpm, `character` sides, `user` map; `isEmpty`), `defaultCategories` / `defaultStyles` (`AttributeList` name + subs: our words), `characterPairs` (9 opposites), `mediaKeyNames` (24), `UserAttribute` (text/number/yesNo, precision), `AttributeFilter` (attribute + values: any, Character all), `MediaQuery::attributes`, `MediaItem::tags` / `tempo()` / `keyName()` (yours over the name's; searchText includes tags). `MediaDatabase`: `tags` by path (kept when not scanned), `getTags`, `editTags (files, fn)` (one save), `setAttribute (files, id, text)` (category/style drop a foreign sub; tempo 20-400; rating via `setRatings`; character by a side's word; user: number rounded to its precision (JUCE's 0 decimals means all), yes/no "1"/"0"), `removeAttribute` (category/style take their sub), `setCharacter (files, pair, side)` (pairs' order kept), `attributeValues (item, id)` (as stored; tempo "120"), `getCategories` / `getStyles` (ours + `userCategories` / `userStyles`), `subsOf`, `addCategory` / `addStyle`, `addUserAttribute` (unique, not one of ours) / `removeUserAttribute` (values go), `passes`, `valueCounts (query, id)` (the query without that attribute's own filter: linked subs), MediaColumn category/style. XML: Tags, UserCategory/UserStyle (Sub), UserAttribute. MainView: `mediaRack` (made the first time it's shown), `rightZoneShown` / `rightZoneWidth` (settings `rightZoneShown`, `rightZoneWidth` 300-720, default 360; at most half the middle), `RightZoneEdge`, `toggleRightZone` / `setRightZoneShown` (Ctrl+Alt+R in handleKey before Alt+R; command Window.RightZone; IMPORT item 7), laid out right after the Inspector (the whole height), `trackUnder (x, y)` (the track list's or arrangement's row), `rackDropped` (instrument: new track; effect: new FX channel), `mediaDropped` applies a track preset onto the track under it (`TrackActions::loadPresetFrom`; failing: a new track). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_RACK=home|loops|instruments`.   - **The file browser tree (0.22.1):** MediaDatabase `exclusions` (left-out folders, saved as `Exclude`), `includedBy (file, locations, exclusions)` (the deepest scanned or left-out folder holding it decides), `isIncluded`, `ScanState` none/some/all and `scanState (folder)` (some: something deeper decides otherwise), `setIncluded (folder, scanned)` (what was decided inside it goes; added as a location or exclusion only if the rule doesn't already give it; `dropUnincluded`; the caller rescans), `scanAll` skips files not included; `addLocation` clears an equal exclusion. MediaBayPage `Browser` (a TreeView; hidden open `Root`; `RootItem`s SCANNED FOLDERS / FAVORITES / THIS COMPUTER / USER CONTENT, filled when opened, `refill`; `FolderItem` (box at x < 18, the folder shape, label coloured by state; lazy `load` of non-hidden subfolders; `refreshViews`); `refreshLists` (refills the first two roots, notes and reopens what was open: JUCE's openness state didn't survive the refill), `reveal (folder)` (opens the way from the root holding it), `showFolderMenu`), `selectedFolder` (query.folder), `selectFolder`, `setFolderScanned`, `refreshFolder`, `addFavorite` / `getFavorites` (settings `mediaRackFavorites`, else `localFavorites`), `revealFolder`, `getFolderChildren`; `removeKey` is REFRESH; `locationsWidth` 300; the status line says when the chosen folder isn't scanned; the timer repaints the tree every 10 frames while scanning. MediaRack: `addFavorite` uses `isIncluded`/`setIncluded`; the timer re-reads `mediaRackFavorites` (favorites from the window). **A TreeView lays out its rows on the message loop:** the snapshot runs `runDispatchLoopUntil (60)` first. Manual pages: File Browser Section, Scanning Folders, Rescanning Media Folders / Refresh Views.
  - **0.22.2:** MediaBay `read`: the reader's `metadataValues` (WAV ACID: `WavAudioFormat::acidTempo/Beats/RootSet/RootNote/OneShot`; AIFF Apple loop: `AiffAudioFormat::appleBeats/RootSet/RootNote/Key/OneShot`; tempo from beats / seconds when missing; none for one-shots), `keyFromRootNote (note, mode)`, `info` (bext description, INFO genre/comments/keywords/subject; searched; saved), `readIXml` (our own RIFF walk: JUCE keeps only iXML's ASWG part) + `tagsFromIXml` (`<BWFXML><STEINBERG><ATTR_LIST><ATTR><NAME/><VALUE/>`, names matched by what they hold: category/sub, style/sub, character, comment, tempo/bpm, key; "A | B" takes B) into `MediaItem::fileTags`, moved into `tags` by `takeScanResults` when the file has none of yours. Priority: tempo yours > file > name; key yours > name > file. `MediaItem::readVersion` / `currentReadVersion` 2 (saved as `read`; older items read again by `scanAll`). MediaBayPage: `saveBrowserState` / `loadBrowserState` (settings `mediaBayTreeOpen` (open items' keys a line each: "root:<heading>" or "<heading>|<path>") and `mediaBayFolder`; without settings a static for the run), saved on every opening/closing (`Browser::opennessChanged`, not while `updating`) and `selectFolder`; restored in the constructor (`restoreOpenKeys`, `showSelected`: chosen with dontSendNotification and scrolled to); `isFolderOpenInTree`.   - **0.23.0 (previews in time):** `cmake/Dependencies.cmake` fetches Signalsmith Stretch 1.4.0 (MIT; it fetches signalsmith-linear 0.6.4), linked PRIVATE to PR7SCore; `engine/Stretch.h/.cpp` `stretchAudio (input, rate, outputLength, semitones)` (`exact()`: the whole sound, its latency handled; the header is `signalsmith-stretch/signalsmith-stretch.h`; that one file compiles with warnings off: `-w` / `/W0`). Engine `setPreviewSync (waitForPlay, anchorBeat, lockBeats)` / `isPreviewWaiting`: waiting = silent while stopped (position back to 0); locked = each segment of the block reads at `fmod ((beat - anchor) / lockBeats, 1)` of the sound, going round. Studio `MediaPreviewOptions` (linkTempo, waitForPlay, semitones, toKey, speed; settings `mediaBayLinkTempo`, `mediaBayWaitForPlay`, `mediaBaySemitones`, `mediaBayToKey`, `mediaBaySpeed`), `getMediaPreviewOptions` / `setMediaPreviewOptions`, `planMediaPreview (file, seconds)` (`MediaPreviewPlan`: lengthFactor, semitones, lockBeats, songBpm at the playhead; the file's tempo/beats/key from the MediaBay item, else its name; beats rounded to whole when within 5%; to key the short way), `keyPitchClass`; `previewMedia` stretches audio ahead (up to 90 s), mono made stereo, peaks again; locks from `project.loopStart` when waiting and linked; MIDI previews take semitones and speed. UI: MediaBayPage `linkKey`, `waitKey`, `halfKey`, `doubleKey`, `transposeBox`, `toKeyBox`, `setPreviewOptions` (plays again if playing), the waiting text; MediaRack `linkKey`, `waitKey`, `pitchKey` (`showPitchMenu`), `setPreviewOptions`. Studio `PluginPreset` (plugin, state, name, instrument), `readPluginPreset` (`DawProject::vstPresetToHostState` for the state; the plug-in among instruments, effects and the studio synth by VST3 `uniqueId`, else by name; the error names the plug-in), `addTrackFromPluginPreset` (instrument: `addTrack` with `Instrument::state`, renamed to the preset; effect: `addFxChannel` then the effect's `setStateInformation`), `applyPluginPreset (trackId, file, error)` (instrument: same plug-in → its state set, `changed()`; else `changeInstrument` with the state; effect: into the same effect in the track's inserts, else `createEffect` with `Insert::state` + `addInsert`; 0 = the master).   - **Controller lanes (0.25.0):** `Controller` (Project.h: type cc/pitchbend/aftertouch/polyPressure/programChange, number, start (part beats), value (0-127; pitchbend 0-16383, 8192 none), ramp (from the event before of its kind)), `Part::controllers` (sorted; XML `Controller` kind/start/value/ramp), `Track::controllerLanes` (codes; default {velocity}; attribute `controllerLanes` when not default; not undo steps) and `pitchbendUp/Down/Snap/Grid`. `engine/Controllers.h/.cpp` (`ControllerKinds`: codes -1 velocity, 0-127 CC, 128 pitchbend, 129 aftertouch, 130 program, 256+note poly; `name`/`shortName`/`ccName`/`valueText`/`maxValue`/`alwaysStep` (the manual's step list)/`toText`/`fromText`/`commonCodes`/`usedCodes`/`valueAt`/`toMidi`/`expandRamps (part, resolution, emit)`). Engine `update`: controllers into the track's events (ramps every 1/128 beat where the value changes; same beat: note off, controllers, note on), `schedule`: held notes only from 0x80/0x90, `bent`/`sustained` noted; `chase` (play start, jump, cycle wrap) -> `chaseControllers` (the last CC/bend/pressure/program before the beat, fixed arrays); `resetControllers` on stop (bend centre, sustain off on the track's channel); `endNotesNoLongerEnded` looks at notes only; `RecordedEvent::pass` (cycleCount) and controllers recorded. Studio `setControllers` (one undo step; sorted, clamped), `setControllerLanes`, `setPitchbendGrid`, `get/setNewControllersRamp` (settings `controllerRamp`, default on: our choice), `buildRecordingControllers` (each kind's last pass; alwaysStep -> step), `cutPart` splits them (a ramp crossing the cut: an end point a 960th before it, the second half starts at the value reached), glue merges, `importMidi` takes `MidiImport::Track::controllers`. UI: `KeyEditor` `Tool::line` / `LineMode` (line, parabola, sine, triangle, square, paint; settings `midiEditorLineMode`), `lineTool` / `lanesKey` in the panel, lanes stacked above the scroll bar (`laneHeight` settings `midiEditorLaneHeight`, squeezed so the notes keep 60 px; `lanesTop`, `getLaneArea`, `getLaneHeader`, `getVelocityArea` = the velocity lane's), KeyEditorLanes.cpp: menus (`showLaneMenu`, `showLanesMenu`), gestures controllerDraw/Erase/Band/Move/Handle/Line, laneResize (divider: 4 px above `lanesTop`), `commitControllers` (sorts with the selection), `drawLine`, `applyVelocityLine`, `getControllerBox` / `controllerHandleAt` / `dragControllerHandle` (the velocity handles' math), `showControllerEntry` (CallOutBox: VALUE, AT, RAMP; `enterControllerValue`, `parseControllerValue`), `copySelectedControllers` / `pasteControllers` (`lanesFocused`: the last click was in a lane; Ctrl+C/X/V, Delete, Ctrl+A follow it). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_LANES=1`. Tests `tests/ControllerTests.cpp`.   - **Hitpoints and warping (0.32.0):** `WarpMarker` (beat from the clip's start, seconds into its file), `Part::warp` (sorted; `isWarped` = 2 or more; the edges at 0 and length; XML `Warp`), `Project::HitpointSettings` (threshold 0.25, minimumSeconds 0.06), `hitpointEdits` (per file: added/removed seconds), `audioWarpQuantize` (XML `Hitpoints`). `engine/Warp.h/.cpp`: `detectHitpoints` (spectral flux, 1024/256 Hann, log1p(100|X|), peaks over the 8-frame mean x1.3 + 2% of the loudest, placed where the 32-sample envelope reaches a fifth of the stretch's loudest, kept only if louder after than before: offsets aren't onsets), `filterHitpoints` (threshold, strongest-first minimum length, hand edits within 10 ms), `warpSecondsAt` / `warpBeatAt` (linear in song seconds between markers, own speed outside), `ensureWarp`, `rebaseWarp (old, updated)` (start/length changed, audio kept in place: used by `cutPart`, `updatePart`, `resizePart`, `moveStart`, `moveTakeEdge`), `warpFactors`, `quantizeWarp` (each grid line takes its nearest marker within half a step, in order; the rest in proportion), `WarpRenderer` (per-stretch pieces cached by file/source range/length; own speed copied, else Signalsmith `stretchAudio` with 30 ms before; each stretch after the first also makes 128 samples before its start, faded in over the stretch before: nothing after a marker is stretched with it (no early smear of the next hit)). Engine `WarpSource` (`setWarpSource`): a warped clip plays its rendered sound from offset 0. Studio `getWarpedSound`, `getHitpoints (recording)` (cache `hitpointCache` of every candidate), `getPartHitpoints (partId)` (beats inside the clip), `setHitpointSettings`, `addHitpoint` / `removeHitpoint` / `moveHitpoint` / `resetHitpoints` (changed (false)), `nextHitpoint (trackId, fromBeat, forward)` (heard clips only), `editWarp` (checks: order, 1/256 beat and 5 ms apart, inside the file, edges at the clip's, `audioOffset` = the first's seconds), `createWarpFromHitpoints`, `addWarpMarker`, `moveWarpMarker` (interior only, between neighbours), `removeWarpMarkers` (two left at own speed: none), `setWarpMarkers`, `resetWarp`, `flattenWarp` ("<track> flattened NN.wav", 32-bit), `quantizeAudio` (warp: markers from hitpoints when none, then `quantizeWarp`; else starts to the grid), `setAudioWarpQuantize`; glue and `glueAdjacent` refuse warped clips; `bounceTakes` reads warped sound. ArrangeView `Tool::warp` (WARP, no number key; `warpDown/Drag/Up`, Drag `warpMarker`, `warpMarkerAt` (5 px), a new marker catches the nearest hitpoint within 8 px (Ctrl: none), drags pulled to the grid within 8 px (Ctrl: free), any modifier + click on a marker removes it, Alt-click adds a hitpoint, Shift-click removes one), `paintPart` (waveform by `warpSecondsAt`; the selected clip's hitpoints when `hitpointRoom` 4 px apart; markers orange with handles and factors not 1.00 where there's room; WARP stamped), part menu "Hitpoints and Warp" 18-22, `showHitpointSettings` (CallOutBox: THRESHOLD, MINIMUM LENGTH). MainView: Alt+N/B on an audio track (else velocities), Q also `quantizeAudio`, the quantize menu's AudioWarp Quantize (1000); commands Audio.NextHitpoint / PreviousHitpoint / AudioWarpQuantize / ResetWarp (no default keys: Alt+N/B shared by context). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_WARP=1`.
