  - **Audio Alignment (0.33.0):** `engine/Align.h/.cpp`: `AlignSettings` (precision 0-1, `Flexibility` low/normal/high, maxShiftSeconds `leastShift` 0.01 to `mostShift` 0.5, matchWords, preferShifting, onlyWhereBothPlay; `tolerance()` 2 ms at 100 % to 30 ms at 0 %; `toString`/`fromString`, settings key `audioAlignment`), `AlignSound` (mono, `start` song seconds), `AlignSyncPoint` (reference, target song seconds), `alignSounds (reference, target, from, to, rate, settings, syncs, protectedRanges)` -> `AlignResult` (points (t, g): the target plays at t what it plays now at g; `shiftOnly`/`shift`; `unmetSyncPoints`; `before`/`after` average distance where both play, on the 10 ms grid), `alignedTargetAt`. How: `measure` every 10 ms (`hop`; a 2048 FFT: 24 log bands 80 Hz-8 kHz z-normalised (`shape`), loudness in tens of dB below the loudest, onset = rise, active within 40 dB); cost = shape (0.3, Match Words 0.8) + loudness + 2 x onset, 0 where either is silent with only-where-both-play; DTW in the band k = j - i in [0, 2W] (W = max shift / hop), steps diagonal, target x2 (k-1), target x0.5 (i-2, k+1; the skipped frame's cost added), each non-diagonal step `penalty` = flexibility factor (1 / 0.35 / 0.1) x the mean active cost; sync points pin their frame's k (within 1), checked in order and at a pace 0.55-1.8 first; open start and end; smoothed by a moving average (1 + 15 x (1 - precision) frames each side), protected ranges held at their mean, sync offsets exact; Prefer Time Shifting: the median offset when 80 % are within reach, refined by waveform cross-correlation (+/- hop + 3 ms) over the busiest 2 s; else Douglas-Peucker (tolerance) between forced points (ends, syncs, protected edges), each kept point placed by `placeExactly` (1 ms envelopes, +/- 12 ms, correlation over 0.6), then paced (20 ms apart, slope 0.4-2.5; points that must stay win). Studio: `AlignReference` (track, beats), `AlignTarget` (part id, optional range limits), `AlignSync` (part id, beats), `AlignReport` (aligned/moved/stretched, `unmetSyncs`, `alignedParts` per target (0: not), before/after, notes), `alignAudio` (refuses the reference's track, frozen, non-audio, no overlap of 0.1 s; a warped target is reset first (noted); split at the overlap with `cutPart` (fades 0.01 s at the cuts); `heardMono` of the reference's track, `clipMono` of the piece (its recording past its edges); markers via `tidyWarp` (editWarp's checks, shared), or a shift into `audioOffset` (negative: the clip starts later); a failed target's parts put back; one `changed()`), `getAlignSettings`/`setAlignSettings`. UI `AudioAlignmentPanel` / `AudioAlignmentWindow` (ui; its own dark page as the Automation panel; `View` (reference lane over target lane, normalised peaks per lane, bars, the overlap, protected hatching, sync lines pearl / red when unmet; mouse: drag reference->target = sync, Ctrl-drag on target = protected, Shift-click removes), SET REFERENCE / ADD TARGETS (the arrangement's range first, else its selected parts) / CLEAR, target box, MATCH WORDS, PREFER TIME SHIFTING, ONLY WHERE BOTH PLAY, LOW/NORMAL/HIGH, PRECISION, MAX SHIFT, CLEAR SYNC AND PROTECTED, ALIGN AUDIO (targets and syncs follow to the aligned pieces), `getReportText`; test API `setReferenceFromSelection`, `addTargetsFromSelection`, `addSyncPoint`, `addProtectedRange`, `removeAt`, `alignNow`, `keyNamed`, `xForBeat`/`beatForX`, `getView`). MainView `toggleAudioAlignment` (`alignmentWindow`), ArrangeView `onOpenAudioAlignment` (part menu 23, range menu 22), command Window.AudioAlignment (no key). `"[snapshot-align]"` (`PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_TAB=before`). Tests `tests/AlignTests.cpp`. Manual pages: Audio Alignment, Audio Alignment Panel, Aligning the Audio of Events. Not: pitch matching, several references, the Sample Editor's clip display.
  - **VariAudio, part one (0.34.0):** `Project::VariSegment` (start/end seconds into the recording, `pitch` (MIDI, fractional; the heard average), `shift`, `straighten` 0-1, `volumeDb`; `pitchNow`, `isEdited`) and `Project::VariAudio` (segments in time order, `bypass`), `Project::variAudio` by file (XML `VariAudio file bypass` > `Segment`; `restore` copies it: undo). `engine/VariAudio.h/.cpp`: `PitchAnalysis` (frames every `hopSeconds` 0.005: midi (0 unvoiced), clarity, levelDb; `pitchAt`), `analysePitch` (YIN with FFT correlation: window 1024 (2048 above 60 kHz), 60-1100 Hz, threshold 0.15 then the dip's bottom, else the deepest; unvoiced over 0.3; parabolic; single frames more than 6 semitones off their 5-frame median replaced), `segmentPitch` (voiced within 40 dB of the loudest; gaps of 2 frames bridged; a cut where the 50 ms medians either side differ by over 0.8 semitone, at the steepest point, the piece after at least 60 ms; a piece under 120 ms whose ends differ by over 0.8 joins the next (scoops); none under 60 ms; pitch = the median of the middle 70 %), `editedPitchAt` / `pitchChangeAt` (pitch + shift + (heard - pitch) x (1 - straighten); a frame over 3 semitones off counts as the pitch), `PitchSnap` absolute/relative/off, `movedShift`, `correctedShift`, `quantizedShift (seg, Key, snap)`, `suggestScales` (duration-weighted pitch classes: in - 1.5 out + tonic 0.3 + fifth 0.15 + the last note's 0.1 x total; 24 keys), `renderVariAudio` (regions = edited segments +/- 30 ms, merged when they meet; each through `shiftPitchAlong` (Stretch.cpp: Signalsmith with `setFormantFactor (1, true)`, the transposition set every 128 samples for the input a latency behind, 0.25 s of real sound before as pre-roll, aligned by the latency); crossfaded in over 10 ms at the region's ends; volume ramped 5 ms; everything else copied). Studio: `editVariAudio` (analyse + segment once; an undo step; SessionLog note), `getVariAudio`, `getPitchAnalysis` (cache `pitchCache` by the AudioData it came from), `setVariSegments` (clamped, sorted, one undo step), `editVariSegments (file, indices, fn)`, `setVariAudioBypass` (changed (false)), `removeVariAudio`, `getHeardAudio` (the recording as edited, cached in `heardCache` by `variSignature` (the edited segments' values; empty when none or bypassed), peaks made again), `auditionRecording (file, from, to, loop)` (a slice through the engine's preview). ArrangeView `onEditAudio (partId)` (double-click an audio clip; part menu 24), MainView `openVariAudio` (one window, `setPart`), command Audio.VariAudio. `"[snapshot-variaudio]"`. Manual pages: Pitch Editing and Time Correction with VariAudio, VariAudio Inspector Section, Segments and Gaps, Segmenting Monophonic Audio, Sample Editor Piano Keyboard Display, Navigating and Zooming through Segments, Auditioning, Smart Controls, Selecting a Musical Scale, Pitch Changes, Pitch Snap Mode, Raising or Lowering Pitches, Correcting Pitches, Quantizing Segment Pitches to Musical Scales (and to the Chord Track), Snapping Segment Pitches While Editing, Straightening Pitch Curves. Part two: 0.35.0.
  - **0.34.1 (speed, safe fixes):** `AutomationLane::valueOf (points, beat, step)` (the interpolation shared; `trimDb` reads the trim in place: it built a lane copying the points on the audio thread). `juce::ScopedNoDenormals` in `Engine::audioDeviceIOCallbackWithContext`, `Engine::render`, `stretchAudio`, `shiftPitchAlong` (values under ~1e-38 to 0: no denormal slow-downs; per thread, scoped). `recordUndoState` moves the plug-in states (and `drumMaps`, `hitpointEdits`, which `restore` never reads) out of `project` while copying, then back. `forgetAudio (name)` / `forgetAllAudio()` (Studio, private) replace `audioCache.erase` / `.clear()`: hitpoint, pitch, heard and warped-signature caches and the warp renderer go too; `hitpointCache` keyed by name and the `AudioData` it came from (a same-named file in another song was served the old hitpoints). `WarpRenderer` counts `whole` (`wholeSamplesKept`, `wholeSamplesHeld`; cleared over 100 M samples or 64 entries). MainView `onScrollOrZoom` lays out the track list only when `scrollY` or `rowZoom` changed (`trackListScrollY` / `trackListRowZoom`). Tests `tests/PerformanceTests.cpp`. **The review's other findings, not done yet:** Studio `changed()` -> `engine.update` rebuilds every track on every fader/pan/send move (per-track `Prepared` cache, mix-only change kind); Engine `playClips` per-sample with branches (vectorise the middle), clip sample positions per block (compute in update), `mixAt` reads every lane (pick mix lanes in update), `endNotesNoLongerEnded` / `chaseControllers` linear scans, unmoving faders/sends per sample, per-block `findTrack`; UI: every change repaints everything (change kinds), ArrangeView `timeline.layout` recomputed per track and part in paint, lanes drawn their whole length per strip, mixer send slots repainted every frame, the transport's pearl and shadow blurs rebuilt 30 Hz, timers while hidden, no `setOpaque`; memory: mono recordings stored stereo, parallel song open.
  - **0.34.2 (speed, the engine):** `TrackState::BuiltFrom` (message thread only: valid, parts, midiChannel, soloLane, the drum map copied): `update` keeps a MIDI track's events when all equal (`Prepared::eventsKept`: no swap, no `checkHeld`), else builds and records them; disabled/frozen/audio tracks invalidate it; a new TrackState starts invalid; `getEventBuilds` (tests). `Clip::startSample/endSample/fromSample/toSample` (`placeClip` in `update`, the same `Timing`); `playClips` plays `[from, to)` with `playOne` (the old per-sample code) at the edges and `FloatVectorOperations::add` over the middle (inside the recording, outside both fades: gain exactly 1). `mixAt` skips non-mix targets before `playedAt`; the per-block screen readout skips `mixAt` without `mixAutomated`. Unmoving `SmoothedValue`s (tracks, master): `FloatVectorOperations::multiply` by the target; sends with `gain == lastGain`: a constant-gain loop. `endNotesNoLongerEnded`: one walk with `unsettled`/`ends` bitsets, note-offs in note order as before. Tests in `tests/PerformanceTests.cpp` (clips bit-exact against the old formula in blocks 512/333/97 over a tempo ramp; events kept after a fader move, built once after a note edit), `"[perf-update]"` (hidden: the timing). Not done: `chaseControllers` snapshots, cached `findTrack` per block, the click/meter/preview small items.
  - **0.34.3:** **DAWproject:** `read` sets `nextTrackId` above every imported id (it stayed 1: `addTrack` reused id 1 and `findTrack` found the imported track); `Studio::openProject` makes sure of it for any song. Reader: an audio track whose channel holds an instrument (role "instrument", or no role and a known instrument plug-in) is an instrument track (Cubase's rack instruments: a MIDI track into an audio channel holding the instrument); `mergeIntoInstruments` (a MIDI track without an instrument whose destination is a track with one: that track in the first MIDI track's place, its parts, name and colour when one feeds it; several: all their parts, a note; their trackIdOf entries point at it, their parameters dropped); `findImplicitMaster` (no role="master": the audio track the most channels play into, with no destination of its own and no content on the arrangement (`hasContent`); without any routing, an empty one named Stereo Out / Main Out / Master / Output) becomes the master's volume, inserts, automation; `noteStudioSynths` after the merge. Tests: `tests/DawProjectTests.cpp` rack and Stereo Out cases (shapes guessed from the format, not a real Cubase file). **Screen:** `Studio::getChangeVersion` (`announce` bumps it unless only `mixOnly (true)` asked: setVolume/setPan/setSendLevel/setMasterVolume without automation written); ArrangeView, TrackList, MixerView, TransportBar, MainView skip a change they've seen (`seenChange`); `SendSlot::repaintIfChanged` (`Shown`); KeyEditor's and MixerView's timers stop while they or a parent are hidden.
  - **0.34.4:** ArrangeView `rowsNow (made)` (the paint's own `rows` through `rowsInPaint` while painting; else a fresh layout): trackTop, rowAt, partRow, getLaneArea, getTempoArea, getChordArea. Paint skips lane/tempo/chord rows outside the clip by `rowMargin` 40. `paintAutomation`: `curveOf` collects each stretch's corners (`Run`), then keeps those inside [clip - `curveMargin` 64, clip + 64] and the one either side (bent ramps' 3 px samples computed only near the window, same accumulation), so every segment crossing the window is the same; Trim's played curve every 3 px from the last value before the window to the first after it; dashes from a multiple of 6 inside the window; points outside skipped. paintTempo's off line likewise. TransportBar `paint`: a screen whose `LedDisplay::screenReach` (20 x size) doesn't touch the clip isn't painted, its `LedDisplay::glassOf` still noted for `paintOverChildren`. **JUCE's antialiased edges differ by a shade where a redraw cuts a line**, so a strip render isn't bit-identical to a whole one, even before this: tests compare strips with whole within 24 levels, and `PARAGON_STRIPS=<folder>` saves the strip images to compare two builds exactly (`tests/AutomationEditTests.cpp` "0.34.4" cases; old vs new: 13 images, 0 differ).
  - **0.34.5 (multi-core audio):** `AudioWorkers` (engine/AudioWorkers.h/.cpp): `run (count, task)` (each task once; the caller takes tasks too and waits only for those helpers began; claims by one atomic `state` = generation << 32 | next index, `jobs[2]` by generation so a late helper can't claim the next job's task; `done`; helpers woken by `std::atomic::wait/notify` on `wake`, spinning `spinSeconds` 0.2 ms first; `setHelpers` stops and starts them; `helpersForThisComputer` = physical cores - 1, at most 15; helpers `markAudioThread` and `ScopedNoDenormals`; Windows: `startThread (highest)` + MMCSS "Pro Audio" (avrt.dll by hand; JUCE's realtime thread would set the whole process REALTIME_PRIORITY_CLASS), else `startRealtimeThread`). Engine: `processTrack` (a track's own work: live MIDI, clips/instrument, inserts with its own `TrackState::effectMidi`, the pre-fader copy `preFaderCopy` when it has a pre-fader send, fader/pan, post inserts, `blockPeak`, `playedThisBlock`) and `mixTrackOut` (audio thread, tracks' order: pre sends from the copy, post sends, into its group or the master, meters); `renderBlock`: per role, `wave` (room reserved by the message thread wherever `tracks` grows; too small: one by one) through `workers.run`, then `mixTrackOut` in order; one by one with no helpers, under 2 tracks, or an FX channel with a send (the studio never makes one: `setSend` refuses FX); `runInserts (.., effectMidi)`, `runSends (.., from)`; `BlockContext`; `setHelperThreads` (under the lock). Studio `getMultiCoreAudio` / `setMultiCoreAudio` (settings `multiCoreAudio`, default on; without devices off), `applyMultiCore` (`PARAGON_HELPERS=<n>` forces it: the whole suite runs on 4 cores with 3), `getAudioHelperThreads`; SessionLog notes it. SETTINGS `multiCore` toggle under FINISH (page 676 high). Tests `tests/MultiCoreTests.cpp` (the pool: 20,000 jobs; a 24-track song bit-identical on 1 and 4 cores; threads seen; FX-into-FX fallback), `"[perf-multicore]"` (hidden: 708 / 405 / 256 ms on 1 / 2 / 4 cores here). **Never share a buffer between tracks' processing** (effectMidi was shared before).

BAR n"), its menu (Insert at the cursor's bar, Add the Signature Track, Tempo Track Editor), the position screen sized by the widest numerator. **Tap tempo:** Studio `TapMode` (automatic/selectionStart/playbackStart/cursor/projectTempo/displayOnly; settings `tapTempoMode`), `tapTempo (nowSeconds, selectionStart)` (the last 9 taps' mean step; `tapGapSeconds` 2 starts again; where chosen at the second tap; applied with `changed (false)`), `endTapping` (the undo step; Studio's timer calls it when the taps stop), `getLastTap`, `isTapping`.   - **Folder tracks (0.50.0):** `Track::Kind::folder` (saved "folder"; `isFolder`; **`isChannel()` is true for folders** (no parts, no instrument) and **`isGroup()` for a folder with `folderGroup`**, so the engine, routing menus and mixer treat it as a group), `Track::folder` (the folder it's in, 0 none; saved), `folderOpen`, `folderGroup`, `folderData` (always/never/hideWhenExpanded). Project `tidyFolders` (invalid or looping folders dropped; every folder's tracks (deep) right after it, by moving tracks; called by fromXml, restore, DAWproject read, every folder action), `isInFolder`, `folderMembers (id, deep)`, `folderDepth`, `isFoldedAway`. Engine `update` sets each channel's role from the song (group / fx: a plain folder is a silent fx-role bus). Studio `addFolderTrack (name, withGroup)`, `moveTracksToNewFolder`, `setTracksFolder` (to the end of the folder's block; 0: out), `setFolderOpen` / `setFolderData` (not undo steps), `setFolderGroup` (on: members' master outputs to it; off: back), `folderGroupOf`, `joinFolderGroup` / `leaveFolderGroup` (private: a track entering / leaving a folder with a group channel), `isFolderSoloed`; `setMute` on a folder mutes its tracks too, `setSolo` on a plain folder solos its tracks (the folder's own solo isn't set: it'd silence FX channels); `moveTracks` sets moved tracks' folder from where they land (under an open folder: in it; else the folder of the track above); `removeTrack` of a folder moves its tracks up (ours); `getShownTracks` / `isTrackShown` skip folded-away tracks (so the track list, Up/Down, ranges); restore copies folder, folderGroup, folderData then tidies. DawProject: a folder Track with children -> a folder track (`currentFolder` while reading them), a submix with children -> a folder with its group channel; `tidyFolders` at the end.   - **0.59.0:** **Groups into groups:** `Project::canRouteTo (trackId, groupId)` (0 always; else a group channel, not itself, whose own way to the master never passes through the track: no loops) and `hasOutput` (not a channel, or a group); `Studio::setOutput` uses it (FX channels still to the master); `Project::fromXml` resets an output it refuses (a loop opened: one side back to the master). Engine: `TrackState::groupHeight` (the groups between it and the master, worked out each block with a guard) and `renderBlock` passes: sources, groups deepest first (one height a pass, a wave per height on the cores), FX; `mixTrackOut` sends a group's sound into its group's bus (not FX). UI: MixerView's OUT and `showOutputMenu`, the Inspector's OUT, TrackList's group menu Output submenu offer only `canRouteTo` groups. **The composer's drag-out:** `DragOut` `newDragOutFolder (root)`, `composerDragFolder()` (temp/"Paragon PR7-S Composer", cleared with the parts' folder; not refused by MainView's `isInterestedInFileDrag`, so a lane dropped on the arrangement lands on the track), Studio `writeLineForDrag (idea, spans, channel, name, error)` (`makePartMidiFile` of a part over the spans), ComposerPage `mouseDown` / `mouseDrag` (6 px: `dragLaneOut`) / `mouseUp` (no drag: HEAR/STOP), `dragLaneOut (layer)` (drums channel 10, else the lane's track's; "Composer <layer> - <style>.mid"; `onExternalDrag` for tests), the hint over the lanes.   - **0.60.0:** **Routing as the other DAW's** (its manual's Routing Section: output busses for audio, group and effect channels; Using Group Channels): `Project::reaches (from, to)` (down outputs and every send target), `canRouteTo (track, destination)` (any `hasOutput` track (not a channel, a group or an FX channel) to 0, a group or an FX channel, not itself, not one that `reaches` back), `canSendTo` (any `hasOutput` track, FX channels too, to an FX channel that doesn't reach back); `Studio::setSend` checks it with the slot's own target taken out first; `fromXml` drops outputs and sends that would loop. Engine: `TrackState::height` worked out in `update` under the lock (relaxation over outputs and sends between channels, at most a pass per track), `deepestChannel`; `renderBlock` passes: sources, then all channels by height from the deepest (a wave per height; none of a pass feeds another of it, so the old FX-into-FX one-by-one fallback went); `mixTrackOut` routes into any non-source channel's bus. UI: MixerView `showOutputMenu` (GROUP CHANNELS / FX CHANNELS sections), `showSendMenu` for FX strips ("would loop back" greyed), FX strips' send slots; Inspector SENDS and OUT for FX; TrackList Output submenus list groups and FX.   - **The riff generator (0.85.0):** `engine/RiffGenerator.h/.cpp` (the header explains it): `RiffFlavour` any/chugs/gallop/tritone ("TRITONE CHUG"; "TRITONE" still read)/creep/stabs/octaves/stopStart/openChords, `RiffRecipe` (style, flavour, section, bars, busy, seed), `generateRiffBars` (riff characters a sixteenth each), `riffFingerprint`, `generateRiff` (through `riffFromBars`, split out of `styleRhythmIdeas` in Riffs.cpp: the written riffs' voicing and velocity code), `riffStepsPerBar`. The general maker: weighted cells by style x flavour x section x metre. **TRITONE CHUG** (`TritoneChug`): the hammer kind (`make`: a call bar = the high tritone chord (`q` b5+5 two octaves up, the owner's grind; `y` b5 doubled; `j` root+b5 high; `n` the grind an octave lower; `^` b9+9 over the chug's root, Flaw's) ringing (or on the bar's last eighth: place 3, chugs `*` muted power chords; answer kind 4: the grind in eighths) + one or two chug cells repeated; bars A A A' answer; answers: power chords on dark degrees (digits `1`-`9` = a power chord that many semitones over the low note), the call creeping, a stop, chugs) and the djent kind (`djent`: `a` the low tritone chord b2/5/8 (upper case rings), `i` its pinch harmonic, groups of 2-6 sixteenths cycling across the bar line, `l`/`m`/`s` low notes bent a half step (`LineNote::Effect::bend`, amount 1)); style weights the kind. BUSY after the riff: fixed chances per sixteenth (thin weak muted hits / fill weak gaps with chugs): monotonic, the beats' hits kept. Composer: `ComposerState::riffFlavour` / `writtenRiffs` (saved as a 9th field), `guitarRecipe` (section = the drums lane's, seed from variation and style), `guitarRiffFingerprint`; `guitarOf` generates unless `writtenRiffs` or a pasted riff. ComposerPage: the guitar's style menu Riff Flavour (`flavourItems` 6000) / Written Riffs (6100), `setRiffFlavour`, `setWrittenRiffs`, `takeNewRiff` (ROLL/GENERATE re-roll while the fingerprint is in `recentRiffs`, 200 kept). Riffs.cpp `riffFromBars`: the walk reads the chord at the next bar line (or the span's end; past the last: the first) and plays a muted power chord when it's the same chord; `%` `&` `!` `+` `;`. `generateRiff` never uses the pedal for DRIVING EIGHTHS (`x` = the chord's root). Shapes drawn without BUSY (4 bars: WWWW / WQHQ / WWWw / TTTT / VVVV / WWVV / DDDD / WDWD); BUSY after: a fixed chance per bar turns W into V (over the middle) or V/T into W (under). A verse: T to W, power chords. Riffs.cpp `riffFromBars`: `nextStrike()` (the next non-hold in this bar, then up to four bars on via `barOf`): `|` and `~` ring to it (and never past their chord's span); `~` voices the chord at the next bar line (root, fifth, octave, third over it); `@`, `$`; a bar beginning `=` with nothing ringing past its bar line (the chord changed) strikes `|` there (`firstStrike`). `add()` ends every note 4 % early (a breath before the next strike): ring lengths are 0.96 of the span. **Fixed:** `+` / `;` (DRIVING EIGHTHS' picked bars) were played at the muted velocity (a symbol has no upper case): open now. `generateRiff` never uses the pedal for DRONING. - `PR7SCore` (CMake) is a static library with everything but `Main.cpp`, so the app and the tests run the same code.

## Build, test, check

- **Build:** `cmake -B build && cmake --build build -j8`. JUCE 9.0.4 (patched after fetching: `cmake/PatchJuce.cmake`) and Catch2 are fetched by CMake (locally you can point `FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_JUCE` at another checkout).
- **Tests:** `xvfb-run -a build/tests/ParagonPR7STests`. All must pass. Engine tests use `Probe` (tests/TestHelpers.h), an instrument that logs every MIDI event with its sample: check timing to the sample, not just "it played". Audio tests (`tests/AudioTests.cpp`) use `ramp` recordings (every sample told apart) and render with an input buffer. Mixer tests (`tests/MixerTests.cpp`) use `SteadyTone` (a DC level per side) and `TestEffect` (`timesEffect`, `plusEffect`, mono ones) and read levels off the output.
- **Render:** `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT=studio.png xvfb-run -a build/tests/ParagonPR7STests "[snapshot]"` (a demo song, with the key editor open on the lead part). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_WIDTH=3000` for 2x, `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_FINISH=black|neon`, `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_ZONE=mixer` for the mixer (=none for no lower zone; the demo has a guitar audio track), `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_FONT=inter|titles` for the other font choices (stand-in effects named after the family's plugins). `"[snapshot-window]"` renders the key editor's own window (`PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_SIZE`, default 5760x1000: three screens; with `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_ZONE=mixer`, the mixer's window with an eight-track song, default 1920x1000). Look, crop and zoom before claiming a visual change is right.
- No pluginval: it's an app. Windows is built with the static C++ runtime.

## Releases

- Bump `project(ParagonPR7S VERSION x.y.z)` and add a `## x.y.z` CHANGELOG section, then push. CI builds Windows and Linux (macOS left out since 0.53.1: the owner works on Windows and Linux; its lines are commented in build.yml), runs the tests, and publishes a release with `ParagonPR7-S-vX.Y.Z-<OS>.zip` (the app itself).
- About 15 minutes after pushing, check the release has both zips (Windows, Linux) **and its tag is your commit**.
- If a job was cancelled while waiting for a runner (0.39.0's Mac job: no steps, no runner), this session can't re-run it (403): push a small commit at the same version; the release job publishes whatever version isn't released yet.
- A job stuck in a step (0.84.1's Linux job sat 30+ minutes in "Install Linux dependencies"): this session can't cancel it either (403); push a small commit at the same version the same way.
- **A failed Linux build doesn't hold back the release** (the owner, 0.85.0): the release job runs whenever it isn't cancelled and publishes what was built, as long as Windows is there (no Windows: nothing). A later push at the same version adds the Linux zip to that release (`publish=add`). The Linux runner looks names up through Cloudflare's DNS (1.1.1.1; the owner's workaround for "Temporary failure resolving azure.archive.ubuntu.com").
- The owner installs on **Windows** and works in **Cubase** and BeatMaker.

## Open questions (ask, don't guess)

- **ASIO on Windows (settled, 0.4.0):** the ASIO SDK is dual-licensed, proprietary or GPLv3 (since October 2025); PR7-S uses the GPLv3 option. It isn't on GitHub: Windows CI downloads it from steinberg.net/asiosdk, checks a GPL licence text is in it, and passes `PARAGON_ASIO_SDK` (CMake turns on `JUCE_ASIO`). If that fails, the build goes on without ASIO and warns: check the Windows job's log for "ASIO SDK (GPLv3) at" after each release. steinberg.net isn't reachable from the cloud sessions, so this can only be checked in CI.
- A **Cubase Operation Manual** from the owner helps for behaviour and shortcuts (never for names or graphics).

## How the owner likes to work

- **Explain the why**, specifically: what was wrong, what changed, and the trade-offs. Plain language; the owner is a musician, not a programmer.
- **Be honest about limits:** say when something is our design, when a detail is a guess, and what can't be checked here (audio by ear, real devices, Windows).
- **Show it:** a rendered screenshot of every visual change, in all three finishes.
- **"merge it"** means: open a PR from the working branch to main and merge it (merge commit), then reset the working branch to the new main.
- Commit messages explain what and why.
